`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_framerate.c`:

```c
#include "vm_framerate.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "VmFramerate"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

int vm_framerate_init(vm_framerate_t *fr, int target_fps) {
    memset(fr, 0, sizeof(vm_framerate_t));

    fr->target_fps = target_fps;
    fr->current_fps = 0;
    fr->frame_count = 0;
    fr->skip_frames = 0;

    fr->frame_interval.tv_sec = 0;
    fr->frame_interval.tv_nsec = 1000000000L / target_fps;

    atomic_store(&fr->enabled, 1);

    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &fr->last_frame);

    LOGI("Framerate initialized: target=%d fps", target_fps);
    return 0;
}

int vm_framerate_wait_frame(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    if (!atomic_load(&fr->enabled)) {
        return 0;
    }

    struct timespec now;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &now);

    long long elapsed_ns = (now.tv_sec - fr->last_frame.tv_sec) * 1000000000L +
                          (now.tv_nsec - fr->last_frame.tv_nsec);

    long long target_ns = fr->frame_interval.tv_nsec;
    long long sleep_ns = target_ns - elapsed_ns;

    if (sleep_ns > 0) {
        struct timespec sleep_time;
        sleep_time.tv_sec = sleep_ns / 1000000000L;
        sleep_time.tv_nsec = sleep_ns % 1000000000L;
        nanosleep(&sleep_time, NULL);
    }

    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &fr->last_frame);
    fr->frame_count++;

    fr->frame_time_ms = elapsed_ns / 1000000.0f;

    static int last_fps_time = 0;
    if (fr->frame_count - last_fps_time >= fr->target_fps) {
        fr->current_fps = fr->target_fps;
        last_fps_time = fr->frame_count;
    }

    return 0;
}

int vm_framerate_update(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    fr->frame_count++;
    return 0;
}

void vm_framerate_set_target(vm_framerate_t *fr, int target_fps) {
    fr->target_fps = target_fps;
    fr->frame_interval.tv_nsec = 1000000000L / target_fps;
    LOGI("Target FPS set to: %d", target_fps);
}

void vm_framerate_enable(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    atomic_store(&fr->enabled, 1);
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &fr->last_frame);
}

void vm_framerate_disable(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    atomic_store(&fr->enabled, 0);
}

int vm_framerate_get_fps(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    return fr->current_fps;
}

float vm_framerate_get_frame_time(vm_framerate_t *fr) {
    return fr->frame_time_ms;
}

void vm_framerate_stats(vm_framerate_t *fr, int *fps, float *frame_time, int *skipped) {
    if (fps) *fps = fr->current_fps;
    if (frame_time) *frame_time = fr->frame_time_ms;
    if (skipped) *skipped = fr->skip_frames;
}

```

`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_framerate.h`:

```h
#ifndef VM_FRAMERATE_H
#define VM_FRAMERATE_H

#include <time.h>
#include <stdatomic.h>

typedef struct vm_framerate {
    struct timespec last_frame;
    struct timespec frame_interval;
    int target_fps;
    int current_fps;
    int frame_count;
    int skip_frames;
    float frame_time_ms;
    float avg_frame_time_ms;
    atomic_int enabled;
} vm_framerate_t;

int vm_framerate_init(vm_framerate_t *fr, int target_fps);
int vm_framerate_wait_frame(vm_framerate_t *fr);
int vm_framerate_update(vm_framerate_t *fr);
void vm_framerate_set_target(vm_framerate_t *fr, int target_fps);
void vm_framerate_enable(vm_framerate_t *fr);
void vm_framerate_disable(vm_framerate_t *fr);
int vm_framerate_get_fps(vm_framerate_t *fr);
float vm_framerate_get_frame_time(vm_framerate_t *fr);
void vm_framerate_stats(vm_framerate_t *fr, int *fps, float *frame_time, int *skipped);

#endif
```

`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_perfmon.c`:

```c
#include "vm_perfmon.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/resource.h>
#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "VmPerfMon"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

int vm_perfmon_init(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    memset(mon, 0, sizeof(vm_perfmon_t));
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->start_time);
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->last_sample);
    mon->sampling_interval_ms = 1000;
    LOGI("Performance monitor initialized");
    return 0;
}

void vm_perfmon_start(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->start_time);
    mon->sample_count = 0;
    LOGI("Performance monitoring started");
}

void vm_perfmon_stop(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    LOGI("Performance monitoring stopped: samples=%d", mon->sample_count);
}

int vm_perfmon_sample(vm_perfmon_t *mon, vm_perfmon_sample_t *sample) {
    struct timespec now;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &now);

    long long elapsed_ns = (now.tv_sec - mon->last_sample.tv_sec) * 1000000000L +
                          (now.tv_nsec - mon->last_sample.tv_nsec);

    if (elapsed_ns < mon->sampling_interval_ms * 1000000L) {
        return -1;
    }

    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->last_sample);

    struct rusage usage;
    getrusage(RUSAGE_SELF, &usage);

    sample->cpu_cycles = mon->cpu_cycles;
    sample->instruction_count = mon->instruction_count;
    sample->cache_misses = mon->cache_misses;
    sample->cache_hits = mon->cache_hits;
    sample->memory_allocations = mon->memory_allocations;
    sample->memory_frees = mon->memory_frees;
    sample->bytes_allocated = mon->bytes_allocated;
    sample->bytes_freed = mon->bytes_freed;

    sample->cpu_usage_percent = (usage.ru_utime.tv_sec + usage.ru_stime.tv_sec) * 100.0 /
                               (now.tv_sec - mon->start_time.tv_sec + 1);

    sample->memory_usage_mb = usage.ru_maxrss / 1024.0;

    sample->cache_hit_rate = mon->cache_hits > 0 ?
        (double)mon->cache_hits / (mon->cache_hits + mon->cache_misses) * 100.0 : 0.0;

    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &sample->timestamp);
    mon->sample_count++;

    return 0;
}

void vm_perfmon_record_alloc(vm_perfmon_t *mon, size_t bytes) {
    mon->memory_allocations++;
    mon->bytes_allocated += bytes;
}

void vm_perfmon_record_free(vm_perfmon_t *mon, size_t bytes) {
    mon->memory_frees++;
    mon->bytes_freed += bytes;
}

void vm_perfmon_record_io(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    mon->io_operations++;
}

void vm_perfmon_record_context_switch(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    mon->context_switches++;
}

void vm_perfmon_get_summary(vm_perfmon_t *mon, char *buffer, size_t buffer_size) {
    struct timespec now;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &now);

    double elapsed = (now.tv_sec - mon->start_time.tv_sec) +
                    (now.tv_nsec - mon->start_time.tv_nsec) / 1000000000.0;

    snprintf(buffer, buffer_size,
        "=== Performance Summary ===\n"
        "Duration: %.2f seconds\n"
        "Samples: %d\n"
        "Memory: alloc=%llu free=%llu bytes=%llu freed=%llu\n"
        "IO operations: %llu\n"
        "Context switches: %llu\n"
        "Memory usage: %.2f MB\n",
        elapsed,
        mon->sample_count,
        (unsigned long long)mon->memory_allocations,
        (unsigned long long)mon->memory_frees,
        (unsigned long long)mon->bytes_allocated,
        (unsigned long long)mon->bytes_freed,
        (unsigned long long)mon->io_operations,
        (unsigned long long)mon->context_switches,
        0.0
    );
}

void vm_perfmon_reset(vm_perfmon_t *mon) {
    memset(mon, 0, sizeof(vm_perfmon_t));
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->start_time);
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &mon->last_sample);
}
```

`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_pool.c`:

```c
#include "vm_pool.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "VmPool"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

int vm_pool_init(vm_pool_t *pool, size_t total_size, size_t block_size) {
    pool->memory = malloc(total_size);
    if (!pool->memory) {
        LOGI("Failed to allocate pool memory");
        return -1;
    }

    pool->total_size = total_size;
    pool->block_size = block_size;
    pool->block_count = total_size / block_size;
    pool->allocated_count = 0;
    pool->freed_count = 0;

    pool->free_list = (vm_pool_block_t *)pool->memory;
    vm_pool_block_t *current = pool->free_list;

    for (int i = 0; i < pool->block_count - 1; i++) {
        current->next = (vm_pool_block_t *)((char *)current + block_size);
        current->size = block_size;
        current->in_use = 0;
        current = current->next;
    }
    current->next = NULL;
    current->size = block_size;
    current->in_use = 0;

    pool->used_list = NULL;

    LOGI("Pool initialized: total=%zu block=%zu count=%d",
         total_size, block_size, pool->block_count);
    return 0;
}

void *vm_pool_alloc(vm_pool_t *pool, size_t size) {
    if (!pool->free_list) {
        LOGI("Pool exhausted");
        return NULL;
    }

    vm_pool_block_t *block = pool->free_list;
    pool->free_list = block->next;

    block->next = pool->used_list;
    pool->used_list = block;
    block->in_use = 1;
    block->size = size;

    pool->allocated_count++;
    return (void *)(block + 1);
}

void vm_pool_free(vm_pool_t *pool, void *ptr) {
    if (!ptr) return;

    vm_pool_block_t *block = (vm_pool_block_t *)ptr - 1;

    if (pool->used_list == block) {
        pool->used_list = block->next;
    } else {
        vm_pool_block_t *prev = pool->used_list;
        while (prev && prev->next != block) {
            prev = prev->next;
        }
        if (prev) {
            prev->next = block->next;
        }
    }

    block->next = pool->free_list;
    pool->free_list = block;
    block->in_use = 0;

    pool->freed_count++;
}

void vm_pool_destroy(vm_pool_t *pool) {
    if (pool->memory) {
        free(pool->memory);
        pool->memory = NULL;
    }
    pool->free_list = NULL;
    pool->used_list = NULL;
    LOGI("Pool destroyed");
}

void vm_pool_stats(vm_pool_t *pool, int *allocated, int *freed, size_t *used) {
    if (allocated) *allocated = pool->allocated_count;
    if (freed) *freed = pool->freed_count;
    if (used) *used = pool->allocated_count * pool->block_size;
}

void vm_pool_get_stats(vm_pool_t *pool, vm_pool_stats_t *stats) {
    stats->total_size = pool->total_size;
    stats->used_size = pool->allocated_count * pool->block_size;
    stats->free_size = pool->freed_count * pool->block_size;
    stats->block_count = pool->block_count;
    stats->allocated_count = pool->allocated_count;
    stats->freed_count = pool->freed_count;
    stats->fragmentation = pool->allocated_count > 0 ?
        (float)pool->freed_count / pool->allocated_count : 0.0f;
}
```

`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_threadpool.c`:

```c
#include "vm_threadpool.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "VmThreadPool"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

static void *worker_thread(void *arg) {
    vm_threadpool_t *pool = (vm_threadpool_t *)arg;

    while (!atomic_load(&pool->shutdown)) {
        pthread_mutex_lock(&pool->mutex);

        while (!pool->task_queue && !atomic_load(&pool->shutdown)) {
            pthread_cond_wait(&pool->condition, &pool->mutex);
        }

        if (atomic_load(&pool->shutdown) && !pool->task_queue) {
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);
            break;
        }

        vm_task_t *task = pool->task_queue;
        if (task) {
            pool->task_queue = task->next;
            if (!pool->task_queue) {
                pool->task_tail = NULL;
            }
        }

        pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);

        if (task) {
            atomic_fetch_add(&pool->active_tasks, 1);
            task->function(task->arg);
            atomic_fetch_sub(&pool->active_tasks, 1);
            atomic_fetch_add(&pool->completed_tasks, 1);
            free(task);
        }
    }

    return NULL;
}

int vm_threadpool_init(vm_threadpool_t *pool, int thread_count) {
    memset(pool, 0, sizeof(vm_threadpool_t));

    pool->thread_count = thread_count;
    pool->threads = malloc(sizeof(pthread_t) * thread_count);

    pthread_mutex_init(&pool->mutex, NULL);
    pthread_cond_init(&pool->condition, NULL);

    atomic_store(&pool->shutdown, 0);
    atomic_store(&pool->active_tasks, 0);
    atomic_store(&pool->completed_tasks, 0);

    for (int i = 0; i < thread_count; i++) {
        pthread_create(&pool->threads[i], NULL, worker_thread, pool);
    }

    LOGI("Thread pool initialized: threads=%d", thread_count);
    return 0;
}

int vm_threadpool_submit(vm_threadpool_t *pool, void (*function)(void *arg), void *arg) {
    if (atomic_load(&pool->shutdown)) {
        return -1;
    }

    vm_task_t *task = malloc(sizeof(vm_task_t));
    task->function = function;
    task->arg = arg;
    task->next = NULL;

    pthread_mutex_lock(&pool->mutex);

    if (pool->task_tail) {
        pool->task_tail->next = task;
    } else {
        pool->task_queue = task;
    }
    pool->task_tail = task;

    pthread_cond_signal(&pool->condition);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);

    return 0;
}

int vm_threadpool_wait(vm_threadpool_t *pool) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&pool->mutex);
    while (pool->task_queue || atomic_load(&pool->active_tasks) > 0) {
        pthread_cond_wait(&pool->condition, &pool->mutex);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);
    return 0;
}

int vm_threadpool_shutdown(vm_threadpool_t *pool) {
    atomic_store(&pool->shutdown, 1);

    pthread_mutex_lock(&pool->mutex);
    pthread_cond_broadcast(&pool->condition);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);

    for (int i = 0; i < pool->thread_count; i++) {
        pthread_join(pool->threads[i], NULL);
    }

    pthread_mutex_destroy(&pool->mutex);
    pthread_cond_destroy(&pool->condition);

    free(pool->threads);
    pool->threads = NULL;

    LOGI("Thread pool shutdown: completed=%d",
         atomic_load(&pool->completed_tasks));
    return 0;
}

void vm_threadpool_stats(vm_threadpool_t *pool, int *active, int *completed, int *pending) {
    if (active) *active = atomic_load(&pool->active_tasks);
    if (completed) *completed = atomic_load(&pool->completed_tasks);

    if (pending) {
        pthread_mutex_lock(&pool->mutex);
        int count = 0;
        vm_task_t *task = pool->task_queue;
        while (task) {
            count++;
            task = task->next;
        }
        *pending = count;
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&pool->mutex);
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/cpp/perf/vm_threadpool.h`:

```h
#ifndef VM_THREADPOOL_H
#define VM_THREADPOOL_H

#include <pthread.h>
#include <stdatomic.h>

typedef struct vm_task {
    void (*function)(void *arg);
    void *arg;
    struct vm_task *next;
} vm_task_t;

typedef struct vm_threadpool {
    pthread_t *threads;
    int thread_count;
    vm_task_t *task_queue;
    vm_task_t *task_tail;
    pthread_mutex_t mutex;
    pthread_cond_t condition;
    atomic_int shutdown;
    atomic_int active_tasks;
    atomic_int completed_tasks;
} vm_threadpool_t;

int vm_threadpool_init(vm_threadpool_t *pool, int thread_count);
int vm_threadpool_submit(vm_threadpool_t *pool, void (*function)(void *arg), void *arg);
int vm_threadpool_wait(vm_threadpool_t *pool);
int vm_threadpool_shutdown(vm_threadpool_t *pool);
void vm_threadpool_stats(vm_threadpool_t *pool, int *active, int *completed, int *pending);

#endif
```

`app/src/main/cpp/test/vm_test.c`:

```c
#include "vm_test.h"
#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "VmTest"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)
#define LOGE(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_ERROR, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

int vm_test_init(vm_test_t *test, const char *name, const char *suite) {
    test->name = name;
    test->suite = suite;
    test->passed = 0;
    test->failed = 0;
    test->skipped = 0;
    test->assertions = 0;
    test->last_error[0] = '\0';
    return 0;
}

int vm_test_suite_init(vm_test_suite_t *suite, const char *name) {
    suite->name = name;
    suite->tests = NULL;
    suite->test_count = 0;
    suite->passed = 0;
    suite->failed = 0;
    suite->skipped = 0;
    return 0;
}

int vm_test_suite_add(vm_test_suite_t *suite, vm_test_t *test) {
    suite->test_count++;
    suite->tests = realloc(suite->tests, sizeof(vm_test_t) * suite->test_count);
    suite->tests[suite->test_count - 1] = *test;
    return 0;
}

int vm_test_suite_run(vm_test_suite_t *suite) {
    LOGI("=== Running test suite: %s ===", suite->name);

    for (int i = 0; i < suite->test_count; i++) {
        vm_test_t *test = &suite->tests[i];
        LOGI("Running test: %s/%s", test->suite, test->name);

        if (test->failed == 0 && test->skipped == 0) {
            LOGI("  PASSED (%d assertions)", test->assertions);
            suite->passed++;
        } else if (test->skipped > 0) {
            LOGI("  SKIPPED: %s", test->last_error);
            suite->skipped++;
        } else {
            LOGE("  FAILED: %s", test->last_error);
            suite->failed++;
        }
    }

    LOGI("=== Suite '%s' completed: %d passed, %d failed, %d skipped ===",
         suite->name, suite->passed, suite->failed, suite->skipped);
    return suite->failed == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

void vm_test_suite_report(vm_test_suite_t *suite) {
    LOGI("\n=== Test Report: %s ===", suite->name);
    LOGI("Total tests: %d", suite->test_count);
    LOGI("Passed: %d", suite->passed);
    LOGI("Failed: %d", suite->failed);
    LOGI("Skipped: %d", suite->skipped);

    if (suite->failed > 0) {
        LOGI("\nFailed tests:");
        for (int i = 0; i < suite->test_count; i++) {
            vm_test_t *test = &suite->tests[i];
            if (test->failed > 0) {
                LOGI("  - %s/%s: %s", test->suite, test->name, test->last_error);
            }
        }
    }
    LOGI("===========================\n");
}

int vm_test_run_all(vm_test_suite_t *suites, int suite_count) {
    int total_passed = 0;
    int total_failed = 0;
    int total_skipped = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < suite_count; i++) {
        vm_test_suite_run(&suites[i]);
        total_passed += suites[i].passed;
        total_failed += suites[i].failed;
        total_skipped += suites[i].skipped;
        vm_test_suite_report(&suites[i]);
    }

    LOGI("\n=== Overall Test Results ===");
    LOGI("Total suites: %d", suite_count);
    LOGI("Total passed: %d", total_passed);
    LOGI("Total failed: %d", total_failed);
    LOGI("Total skipped: %d", total_skipped);
    LOGI("============================\n");

    return total_failed == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`app/src/main/cpp/test/vm_test.h`:

```h
#ifndef VM_TEST_H
#define VM_TEST_H

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

typedef struct vm_test {
    const char *name;
    const char *suite;
    int passed;
    int failed;
    int skipped;
    int assertions;
    char last_error[256];
} vm_test_t;

typedef struct vm_test_suite {
    const char *name;
    vm_test_t *tests;
    int test_count;
    int passed;
    int failed;
    int skipped;
} vm_test_suite_t;

#define VM_TEST_ASSERT(test, condition, message) do { \
    (test)->assertions++; \
    if (!(condition)) { \
        snprintf((test)->last_error, sizeof((test)->last_error), \
                "Assertion failed: %s at %s:%d", message, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
        (test)->failed++; \
        return; \
    } \
    (test)->passed++; \
} while(0)

#define VM_TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(test, expected, actual, message) do { \
    (test)->assertions++; \
    if ((expected) != (actual)) { \
        snprintf((test)->last_error, sizeof((test)->last_error), \
                "Expected %s to be %d, got %d at %s:%d", \
                message, (int)(expected), (int)(actual), __FILE__, __LINE__); \
        (test)->failed++; \
        return; \
    } \
    (test)->passed++; \
} while(0)

#define VM_TEST_ASSERT_STR_EQUAL(test, expected, actual, message) do { \
    (test)->assertions++; \
    if (strcmp((expected), (actual)) != 0) { \
        snprintf((test)->last_error, sizeof((test)->last_error), \
                "Expected %s to be '%s', got '%s' at %s:%d", \
                message, expected, actual, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
        (test)->failed++; \
        return; \
    } \
    (test)->passed++; \
} while(0)

#define VM_TEST_SKIP(test, message) do { \
    (test)->skipped++; \
    snprintf((test)->last_error, sizeof((test)->last_error), \
            "Skipped: %s", message); \
    return; \
} while(0)

int vm_test_init(vm_test_t *test, const char *name, const char *suite);
int vm_test_suite_init(vm_test_suite_t *suite, const char *name);
int vm_test_suite_add(vm_test_suite_t *suite, vm_test_t *test);
int vm_test_suite_run(vm_test_suite_t *suite);
void vm_test_suite_report(vm_test_suite_t *suite);
int vm_test_run_all(vm_test_suite_t *suites, int suite_count);

#endif
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/VMApp.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine

import android.app.Application
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.os.Build
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.NativeEngine

class VMApp : Application() {

    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "vm_service"
        const val CHANNEL_NAME = "VM Service"
        lateinit var instance: VMApp
            private set
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        NativeEngine.init()
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID,
                CHANNEL_NAME,
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "虚拟机运行服务"
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            manager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    override fun onTerminate() {
        super.onTerminate()
        NativeEngine.destroy()
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/apk/ApkManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.apk

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageInfo
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Environment
import android.util.Log
import java.io.*
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

class ApkManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ApkManager"
        private const val EXPORT_DIR = "VMStudio/ApkExport"
    }

    private val packageManager = context.packageManager

    data class ApkInfo(
        val packageName: String,
        val appName: String,
        val versionName: String,
        val versionCode: Long,
        val installTime: Long,
        val updateTime: Long,
        val size: Long,
        val apkPath: String
    )

    fun getInstalledApks(): List<ApkInfo> {
        val packages = packageManager.getInstalledPackages(0)
        return packages.map { pkg ->
            ApkInfo(
                packageName = pkg.packageName,
                appName = pkg.applicationInfo?.loadLabel(packageManager).toString() ?: pkg.packageName,
                versionName = pkg.versionName ?: "unknown",
                versionCode = if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                    pkg.longVersionCode
                } else {
                    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                    pkg.versionCode.toLong()
                },
                installTime = pkg.firstInstallTime,
                updateTime = pkg.lastUpdateTime,
                size = getFileSize(pkg.applicationInfo?.sourceDir ?: ""),
                apkPath = pkg.applicationInfo?.sourceDir ?: ""
            )
        }.sortedBy { it.appName }
    }

    fun getApkInfo(packageName: String): ApkInfo? {
        return try {
            val pkg = packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0)
            ApkInfo(
                packageName = pkg.packageName,
                appName = pkg.applicationInfo?.loadLabel(packageManager).toString() ?: pkg.packageName,
                versionName = pkg.versionName ?: "unknown",
                versionCode = if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                    pkg.longVersionCode
                } else {
                    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                    pkg.versionCode.toLong()
                },
                installTime = pkg.firstInstallTime,
                updateTime = pkg.lastUpdateTime,
                size = getFileSize(pkg.applicationInfo?.sourceDir ?: ""),
                apkPath = pkg.applicationInfo?.sourceDir ?: ""
            )
        } catch (e: PackageManager.NameNotFoundException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Package not found: $packageName", e)
            null
        }
    }

    fun extractApk(packageName: String): File? {
        return try {
            val appInfo = packageManager.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
            val apkPath = appInfo.sourceDir ?: return null
            val apkFile = File(apkPath)

            if (!apkFile.exists()) return null

            val exportDir = File(
                Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS),
                EXPORT_DIR
            )
            exportDir.mkdirs()

            val timestamp = SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss", Locale.US).format(Date())
            val exportFile = File(exportDir, "${packageName}_${timestamp}.apk")

            apkFile.copyTo(exportFile, overwrite = true)
            Log.i(TAG, "APK extracted to: ${exportFile.absolutePath}")
            exportFile
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to extract APK: $packageName", e)
            null
        }
    }

    fun extractAllApks(): List<File> {
        val extractedFiles = mutableListOf<File>()
        val packages = packageManager.getInstalledPackages(0)

        for (pkg in packages) {
            extractApk(pkg.packageName)?.let { file ->
                extractedFiles.add(file)
            }
        }

        Log.i(TAG, "Extracted ${extractedFiles.size} APKs")
        return extractedFiles
    }

    fun importApk(uri: Uri): Boolean {
        return try {
            val inputStream = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri) ?: return false

            val importDir = File(
                Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS),
                "VMStudio/ApkImport"
            )
            importDir.mkdirs()

            val fileName = getFileNameFromUri(uri) ?: "imported.apk"
            val importFile = File(importDir, fileName)

            inputStream.use { input ->
                importFile.outputStream().use { output ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }

            installApk(importFile)
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import APK", e)
            false
        }
    }

    fun importApkFromPath(path: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            val apkFile = File(path)
            if (!apkFile.exists()) {
                Log.e(TAG, "APK file not found: $path")
                return false
            }

            installApk(apkFile)
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import APK from path", e)
            false
        }
    }

    private fun installApk(apkFile: File): Boolean {
        return try {
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                setDataAndType(
                    Uri.fromFile(apkFile),
                    "application/vnd.android.package-archive"
                )
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)
            Log.i(TAG, "Installing APK: ${apkFile.name}")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to install APK", e)
            false
        }
    }

    fun uninstallApp(packageName: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_UNINSTALL_PACKAGE).apply {
                data = Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)
            Log.i(TAG, "Uninstalling app: $packageName")
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to uninstall app", e)
            false
        }
    }

    fun launchApp(packageName: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            val intent = packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(packageName)
            if (intent != null) {
                intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(intent)
                Log.i(TAG, "Launching app: $packageName")
                true
            } else {
                Log.w(TAG, "No launch intent for: $packageName")
                false
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to launch app", e)
            false
        }
    }

    fun getAppIcon(packageName: String): android.graphics.drawable.Drawable? {
        return try {
            val appInfo = packageManager.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
            appInfo.loadIcon(packageManager)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    private fun getFileNameFromUri(uri: Uri): String? {
        var fileName: String? = null
        if (uri.scheme == "content") {
            val cursor = context.contentResolver.query(uri, null, null, null, null)
            cursor?.use {
                if (it.moveToFirst()) {
                    val nameIndex = it.getColumnIndex(android.provider.OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                    if (nameIndex >= 0) {
                        fileName = it.getString(nameIndex)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (fileName == null) {
            fileName = uri.path?.substringAfterLast('/')
        }
        return fileName
    }

    private fun getFileSize(path: String): Long {
        return try {
            File(path).length()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            0L
        }
    }

    fun getExportDir(): File {
        val dir = File(
            Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS),
            EXPORT_DIR
        )
        dir.mkdirs()
        return dir
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/background/BackgroundManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.background

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.provider.Settings
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.os.IBinder
import android.app.Notification

class BackgroundManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "BackgroundManager"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "vm_studio_channel"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
    }

    private val activityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
    private val powerManager = context.getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager

    fun isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(): Boolean {
        return powerManager.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context.packageName)
    }

    fun requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations() {
        if (!isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations()) {
            val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS).apply {
                data = Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)
            Log.i(TAG, "Requested battery optimization exemption")
        }
    }

    fun hasBackgroundPermission(): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            return true
        }
        return true
    }

    fun requestBackgroundPermission() {
        Log.i(TAG, "Background permission check")
    }

    fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID,
                "VM Studio Service",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "VM Studio virtual machine service"
                setShowBadge(false)
            }

            val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            notificationManager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    fun buildNotification(title: String, content: String): Notification {
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            context, 0,
            context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(context.packageName),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle(title)
            .setContentText(content)
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_manage)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .build()
    }

    fun startForegroundService(service: Class<*>) {
        val intent = Intent(context, service).apply {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(this)
            } else {
                context.startService(this)
            }
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "Started foreground service")
    }

    fun keepAlive() {
        requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations()
        requestBackgroundPermission()
        createNotificationChannel()
        Log.i(TAG, "Keep alive mechanisms activated")
    }

    fun isServiceRunning(serviceClass: Class<*>): Boolean {
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        val services = activityManager.getRunningServices(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
        return services.any { it.service.className == serviceClass.name }
    }

    fun getMemoryInfo(): ActivityManager.MemoryInfo {
        val memInfo = ActivityManager.MemoryInfo()
        activityManager.getMemoryInfo(memInfo)
        return memInfo
    }

    fun isLowMemory(): Boolean {
        val memInfo = getMemoryInfo()
        return memInfo.lowMemory
    }

    fun killBackgroundProcesses(packageName: String) {
        activityManager.killBackgroundProcesses(packageName)
        Log.i(TAG, "Killed background processes for: $packageName")
    }

    fun clearApplicationCache() {
        try {
            val cacheDir = context.cacheDir
            if (cacheDir.exists()) {
                cacheDir.deleteRecursively()
                Log.i(TAG, "Application cache cleared")
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to clear cache", e)
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/background/ForegroundService.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.background

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import android.util.Log

class ForegroundService : Service() {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ForegroundService"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
    }

    private lateinit var backgroundManager: BackgroundManager

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        backgroundManager = BackgroundManager(this)
        Log.i(TAG, "Foreground service created")
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        backgroundManager.createNotificationChannel()
        val notification = backgroundManager.buildNotification(
            "VM Studio",
            "Virtual machine is running"
        )

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        }

        Log.i(TAG, "Foreground service started")
        return START_STICKY
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? {
        return null
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        Log.i(TAG, "Foreground service destroyed")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/camera/CameraService.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.camera

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.ImageFormat
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.hardware.camera2.*
import android.media.ImageReader
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.util.Log
import android.view.Surface

class CameraService(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "CameraService"
    }

    private val nativeCamera = NativeCamera()
    private val cameraManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager
    private var cameraDevice: CameraDevice? = null
    private var captureSession: CameraCaptureSession? = null
    private var imageReader: ImageReader? = null
    private var backgroundThread: HandlerThread? = null
    private var backgroundHandler: Handler? = null
    private var isRunning = false
    private var currentCameraId = "0"

    interface CameraCallback {
        fun onFrameCaptured(data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int)
    }

    private var cameraCallback: CameraCallback? = null

    fun setCallback(callback: CameraCallback) {
        this.cameraCallback = callback
    }

    fun start(cameraId: String = "0", width: Int = 640, height: Int = 480): Boolean {
        if (isRunning) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Camera service already running")
            return true
        }

        currentCameraId = cameraId
        nativeCamera.setPreviewSize(width, height)

        startBackgroundThread()

        imageReader = ImageReader.newInstance(
            width, height, ImageFormat.YUV_420_888, 2
        ).apply {
            setOnImageAvailableListener({ reader ->
                val image = reader.acquireLatestImage() ?: return@setOnImageAvailableListener
                val buffer = image.planes[0].buffer
                val data = ByteArray(buffer.remaining())
                buffer.get(data)

                cameraCallback?.onFrameCaptured(data, image.width, image.height)

                image.close()
            }, backgroundHandler)
        }

        try {
            cameraManager.openCamera(cameraId, object : CameraDevice.StateCallback() {
                override fun onOpened(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    cameraDevice = camera
                    createCaptureSession()
                    isRunning = true
                    Log.i(TAG, "Camera opened: ${camera.id}")
                }

                override fun onDisconnected(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    Log.w(TAG, "Camera disconnected")
                }

                override fun onError(camera: CameraDevice, error: Int) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    Log.e(TAG, "Camera error: $error")
                }
            }, backgroundHandler)

            return true
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Camera permission denied", e)
            return false
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to open camera", e)
            return false
        }
    }

    private fun createCaptureSession() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val surface = imageReader?.surface ?: return

        camera.createCaptureSession(
            listOf(surface),
            object : CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback() {
                override fun onConfigured(session: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    captureSession = session
                    startPreview()
                    Log.i(TAG, "Capture session created")
                }

                override fun onConfigureFailed(session: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Capture session configuration failed")
                }
            },
            backgroundHandler
        )
    }

    private fun startPreview() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val session = captureSession ?: return

        try {
            val captureRequest = camera.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_PREVIEW).apply {
                addTarget(imageReader!!.surface)
                set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_VIDEO)
            }

            session.setRepeatingRequest(captureRequest.build(), null, backgroundHandler)
            Log.i(TAG, "Preview started")
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to start preview", e)
        }
    }

    fun setSurface(surface: Surface) {
        nativeCamera.setSurface(surface)
    }

    fun switchCamera() {
        val newCameraId = if (currentCameraId == "0") "1" else "0"
        stop()
        start(newCameraId)
    }

    fun takePicture() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val session = captureSession ?: return

        try {
            val captureRequest = camera.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_STILL_CAPTURE).apply {
                addTarget(imageReader!!.surface)
                set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
            }

            session.capture(captureRequest.build(), null, backgroundHandler)
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to take picture", e)
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        isRunning = false
        try {
            captureSession?.close()
            captureSession = null
            cameraDevice?.close()
            cameraDevice = null
            imageReader?.close()
            imageReader = null
            stopBackgroundThread()
            nativeCamera.release()
            Log.i(TAG, "Camera service stopped")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error stopping camera", e)
        }
    }

    private fun startBackgroundThread() {
        backgroundThread = HandlerThread("CameraBackground").also { it.start() }
        backgroundHandler = Handler(backgroundThread!!.looper)
    }

    private fun stopBackgroundThread() {
        backgroundThread?.quitSafely()
        try {
            backgroundThread?.join()
            backgroundThread = null
            backgroundHandler = null
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Background thread interrupted", e)
        }
    }

    fun isRunning(): Boolean = isRunning

    fun getCameraList(): List<GuestCameraManager.CameraInfo> {
        val cameras = mutableListOf<GuestCameraManager.CameraInfo>()
        for (id in cameraManager.cameraIdList) {
            val characteristics = cameraManager.getCameraCharacteristics(id)
            val facing = characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING)
            cameras.add(GuestCameraManager.CameraInfo(
                id = id,
                facing = if (facing == CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_FRONT) "Front" else "Back"
            ))
        }
        return cameras
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/camera/GuestCameraManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.camera

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.ImageFormat
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.hardware.camera2.*
import android.media.ImageReader
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Size
import android.view.Surface

class GuestCameraManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "GuestCameraManager"
    }

    private val cameraManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager
    private var cameraDevice: CameraDevice? = null
    private var captureSession: CameraCaptureSession? = null
    private var imageReader: ImageReader? = null
    private var backgroundThread: HandlerThread? = null
    private var backgroundHandler: Handler? = null
    private var previewSurface: Surface? = null
    private var listener: CameraFrameListener? = null

    interface CameraFrameListener {
        fun onFrameAvailable(data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int)
    }

    fun setListener(listener: CameraFrameListener) {
        this.listener = listener
    }

    fun startBackgroundThread() {
        backgroundThread = HandlerThread("CameraBackground").also { it.start() }
        backgroundHandler = Handler(backgroundThread!!.looper)
    }

    fun stopBackgroundThread() {
        backgroundThread?.quitSafely()
        try {
            backgroundThread?.join()
            backgroundThread = null
            backgroundHandler = null
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Background thread interrupted", e)
        }
    }

    fun openCamera(cameraId: String = "0", width: Int = 640, height: Int = 480): Boolean {
        return try {
            startBackgroundThread()

            imageReader = ImageReader.newInstance(
                width, height, ImageFormat.YUV_420_888, 2
            ).apply {
                setOnImageAvailableListener({ reader ->
                    val image = reader.acquireLatestImage() ?: return@setOnImageAvailableListener
                    val buffer = image.planes[0].buffer
                    val data = ByteArray(buffer.remaining())
                    buffer.get(data)
                    listener?.onFrameAvailable(data, image.width, image.height)
                    image.close()
                }, backgroundHandler)
            }

            val characteristics = cameraManager.getCameraCharacteristics(cameraId)
            val streamConfigurations = characteristics.get(
                CameraCharacteristics.SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP
            )
            val sizes = streamConfigurations?.getOutputSizes(ImageFormat.YUV_420_888)
            Log.i(TAG, "Available preview sizes: ${sizes?.joinToString()}")

            cameraManager.openCamera(cameraId, object : CameraDevice.StateCallback() {
                override fun onOpened(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    cameraDevice = camera
                    createCaptureSession()
                    Log.i(TAG, "Camera opened: ${camera.id}")
                }

                override fun onDisconnected(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    Log.w(TAG, "Camera disconnected")
                }

                override fun onError(camera: CameraDevice, error: Int) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    Log.e(TAG, "Camera error: $error")
                }
            }, backgroundHandler)

            true
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Camera permission denied", e)
            false
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to open camera", e)
            false
        }
    }

    private fun createCaptureSession() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val surface = imageReader?.surface ?: return

        camera.createCaptureSession(
            listOf(surface),
            object : CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback() {
                override fun onConfigured(session: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    captureSession = session
                    startPreview()
                    Log.i(TAG, "Capture session created")
                }

                override fun onConfigureFailed(session: CameraCaptureSession) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Capture session configuration failed")
                }
            },
            backgroundHandler
        )
    }

    private fun startPreview() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val session = captureSession ?: return

        try {
            val captureRequest = camera.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_PREVIEW).apply {
                addTarget(imageReader!!.surface)
                set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_VIDEO)
            }

            session.setRepeatingRequest(captureRequest.build(), null, backgroundHandler)
            Log.i(TAG, "Preview started")
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to start preview", e)
        }
    }

    fun takePicture() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val session = captureSession ?: return

        try {
            val captureRequest = camera.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_STILL_CAPTURE).apply {
                addTarget(imageReader!!.surface)
                set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
            }

            session.capture(captureRequest.build(), null, backgroundHandler)
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to take picture", e)
        }
    }

    fun switchCamera() {
        val currentCameraId = cameraDevice?.id ?: "0"
        val newCameraId = if (currentCameraId == "0") "1" else "0"
        closeCamera()
        openCamera(newCameraId)
    }

    fun closeCamera() {
        try {
            captureSession?.close()
            captureSession = null
            cameraDevice?.close()
            cameraDevice = null
            imageReader?.close()
            imageReader = null
            stopBackgroundThread()
            Log.i(TAG, "Camera closed")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error closing camera", e)
        }
    }

    fun getCameraList(): List<CameraInfo> {
        val cameras = mutableListOf<CameraInfo>()
        for (id in cameraManager.cameraIdList) {
            val characteristics = cameraManager.getCameraCharacteristics(id)
            val facing = characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING)
            cameras.add(CameraInfo(
                id = id,
                facing = if (facing == CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_FRONT) "Front" else "Back"
            ))
        }
        return cameras
    }

    data class CameraInfo(
        val id: String,
        val facing: String
    )
}
```

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.camera

import android.view.Surface

class NativeCamera {

    companion object {
        init {
            System.loadLibrary("vmcore")
        }
    }

    external fun setSurface(surface: Surface?)
    external fun setPreviewSize(width: Int, height: Int)
    external fun displayFrame(frameData: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): Boolean
    external fun release()
    external fun getWidth(): Int
    external fun getHeight(): Int
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/ApkManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream

class ApkManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "ApkManager"
    }

    data class ApkInfo(
        val packageName: String,
        val versionName: String,
        val versionCode: Long,
        val label: String,
        val size: Long,
        val filePath: String
    )

    fun installApk(filePath: String, instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        try {
            val file = File(filePath)
            if (!file.exists()) {
                Log.e(TAG, "APK file not found: $filePath")
                return false
            }

            // Copy APK to VM data directory
            val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
            vmDir.mkdirs()
            val destFile = File(vmDir, file.name)
            file.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)

            // Send intent to install
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                setDataAndType(Uri.fromFile(destFile), "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)

            Log.i(TAG, "APK installed: ${file.name}")
            return true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to install APK", e)
            return false
        }
    }

    fun extractApk(packageName: String): String? {
        try {
            val pm = context.packageManager
            val appInfo = pm.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
            val apkPath = appInfo.sourceDir

            val outputDir = File(context.filesDir, "extracted_apks")
            outputDir.mkdirs()

            val outputFile = File(outputDir, "$packageName.apk")
            File(apkPath).copyTo(outputFile, overwrite = true)

            Log.i(TAG, "APK extracted: ${outputFile.absolutePath}")
            return outputFile.absolutePath
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to extract APK", e)
            return null
        }
    }

    fun exportApk(sourcePath: String, destPath: String): Boolean {
        try {
            val sourceFile = File(sourcePath)
            val destFile = File(destPath)
            destFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()
            sourceFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)
            Log.i(TAG, "APK exported: $destPath")
            return true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to export APK", e)
            return false
        }
    }

    fun getInstalledApks(): List<ApkInfo> {
        val pm = context.packageManager
        val packages = pm.getInstalledApplications(PackageManager.GET_META_DATA)

        return packages.mapNotNull { appInfo ->
            try {
                ApkInfo(
                    packageName = appInfo.packageName,
                    versionName = pm.getPackageInfo(appInfo.packageName, 0).versionName ?: "",
                    versionCode = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                        pm.getPackageInfo(appInfo.packageName, 0).longVersionCode
                    } else {
                        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                        pm.getPackageInfo(appInfo.packageName, 0).versionCode.toLong()
                    },
                    label = appInfo.loadLabel(pm).toString(),
                    size = File(appInfo.sourceDir).length(),
                    filePath = appInfo.sourceDir
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                null
            }
        }
    }

    fun getApkInfo(filePath: String): ApkInfo? {
        try {
            val file = File(filePath)
            if (!file.exists()) return null

            val pm = context.packageManager
            val packageInfo = pm.getPackageArchiveInfo(filePath, PackageManager.GET_META_DATA)

            return if (packageInfo != null) {
                ApkInfo(
                    packageName = packageInfo.packageName,
                    versionName = packageInfo.versionName ?: "",
                    versionCode = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                        packageInfo.longVersionCode
                    } else {
                        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                        packageInfo.versionCode.toLong()
                    },
                    label = packageInfo.applicationInfo?.loadLabel(pm)?.toString() ?: "",
                    size = file.length(),
                    filePath = filePath
                )
            } else null
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return null
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/BackgroundManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.app.AlarmManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.util.Log
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class BackgroundManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "BackgroundManager"
        private const val KEEP_ALIVE_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000L
    }

    private val wakeLock: PowerManager.WakeLock by lazy {
        val pm = context.getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager
        pm.newWakeLock(PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "VMStudio::KeepAlive")
    }

    private val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor()
    private var isKeepAliveActive = false

    fun enableKeepAlive() {
        if (isKeepAliveActive) return
        isKeepAliveActive = true

        // Acquire wake lock
        wakeLock.acquire(10 * 60 * 1000L)

        // Schedule periodic keep-alive
        executor.scheduleAtFixedRate({
            try {
                val am = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as ActivityManager
                val isRunning = am.getRunningServices(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
                    .any { it.service.className == "com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmService" }

                if (!isRunning) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Service not running, restarting...")
                    restartService()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Keep-alive check failed", e)
            }
        }, KEEP_ALIVE_INTERVAL_MS, KEEP_ALIVE_INTERVAL_MS, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)

        Log.i(TAG, "Keep-alive enabled")
    }

    fun disableKeepAlive() {
        isKeepAliveActive = false
        executor.shutdownNow()
        if (wakeLock.isHeld) {
            wakeLock.release()
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "Keep-alive disabled")
    }

    private fun restartService() {
        try {
            val intent = Intent(context, Class.forName("com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmService"))
            intent.action = "ACTION_KEEP_ALIVE"

            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                context.startForegroundService(intent)
            } else {
                context.startService(intent)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to restart service", e)
        }
    }

    fun requestIgnoreBatteryOptimization() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            val intent = Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATION_SETTINGS)
            intent.flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
            context.startActivity(intent)
        }
    }

    fun isIgnoringBatteryOptimization(): Boolean {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
            val am = context.getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager
            return am.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context.packageName)
        }
        return true
    }

    fun enableAutoStart() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            Log.i(TAG, "Auto-start requires manual permission grant")
        }
    }

    fun checkAndRequestPermissions(): List<String> {
        val permissions = mutableListOf<String>()

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            // Android 13+ permissions
            permissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
            permissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES)
            permissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_VIDEO)
            permissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO)
        }

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            // Android 12+ permissions
            permissions.add(android.Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT)
        }

        return permissions
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/CameraManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.ImageFormat
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.hardware.camera2.*
import android.media.ImageReader
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.view.Surface
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat

class GuestCameraManager(private val context: Context) {

    private val cameraManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager
    private var cameraDevice: CameraDevice? = null
    private var captureSession: CameraCaptureSession? = null
    private var imageReader: ImageReader? = null
    private var previewSurface: Surface? = null
    private var handlerThread: HandlerThread? = null
    private var handler: Handler? = null
    private var isActive = false
    private var currentCameraId = "0"

    interface CameraFrameCallback {
        fun onFrameAvailable(data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int)
    }

    private val frameCallbacks = mutableListOf<CameraFrameCallback>()

    fun start(cameraId: String = "0", width: Int = 640, height: Int = 480) {
        if (isActive) return
        if (ActivityCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.CAMERA) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            return
        }

        currentCameraId = cameraId
        isActive = true

        handlerThread = HandlerThread("CameraThread").apply { start() }
        handler = Handler(handlerThread!!.looper)

        try {
            val characteristics = cameraManager.getCameraCharacteristics(cameraId)
            val streamConfigMap = characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP)

            imageReader = ImageReader.newInstance(width, height, ImageFormat.YUV_420_888, 2)
            imageReader?.setOnImageAvailableListener({ reader ->
                val image = reader.acquireLatestImage() ?: return@setOnImageAvailableListener
                val buffer = image.planes[0].buffer
                val data = ByteArray(buffer.remaining())
                buffer.get(data)
                image.close()

                frameCallbacks.forEach { it.onFrameAvailable(data, width, height) }
            }, handler)

            cameraManager.openCamera(cameraId, object : CameraDevice.StateCallback() {
                override fun onOpened(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    cameraDevice = camera
                    createCaptureSession(width, height)
                }

                override fun onDisconnected(camera: CameraDevice) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    isActive = false
                }

                override fun onError(camera: CameraDevice, error: Int) {
                    camera.close()
                    cameraDevice = null
                    isActive = false
                }
            }, handler)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            isActive = false
        }
    }

    private fun createCaptureSession(width: Int, height: Int) {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val reader = imageReader ?: return

        try {
            camera.createCaptureSession(
                listOf(reader.surface),
                object : CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback() {
                    override fun onConfigured(session: CameraCaptureSession) {
                        captureSession = session
                        startPreview()
                    }

                    override fun onConfigureFailed(session: CameraCaptureSession) {}
                },
                handler
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun startPreview() {
        val camera = cameraDevice ?: return
        val session = captureSession ?: return
        val reader = imageReader ?: return

        try {
            val request = camera.createCaptureRequest(CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_PREVIEW).apply {
                addTarget(reader.surface)
                set(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_MODE_AUTO)
            }

            session.setRepeatingRequest(request.build(), null, handler)
        } catch (e: Exception) {}
    }

    fun stop() {
        isActive = false
        captureSession?.close()
        cameraDevice?.close()
        imageReader?.close()
        handlerThread?.quitSafely()
        handlerThread = null
        handler = null
    }

    fun registerFrameCallback(callback: CameraFrameCallback) {
        frameCallbacks.add(callback)
    }

    fun unregisterFrameCallback(callback: CameraFrameCallback) {
        frameCallbacks.remove(callback)
    }

    fun switchCamera() {
        val cameraIds = cameraManager.cameraIdList
        if (cameraIds.size <= 1) return
        val nextId = if (currentCameraId == "0") "1" else "0"
        stop()
        start(nextId)
    }

    fun getAvailableCameraIds(): List<String> = cameraManager.cameraIdList.toList()

    fun isActive(): Boolean = isActive
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/FileTransferManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.util.Log
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream

class FileTransferManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "FileTransferManager"
    }

    data class TransferResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val sourcePath: String,
        val destPath: String,
        val size: Long,
        val message: String = ""
    )

    fun importFile(sourceUri: Uri, instanceId: Int, destPath: String): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
            val destFile = File(vmDir, destPath)
            destFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()

            context.contentResolver.openInputStream(sourceUri)?.use { input ->
                FileOutputStream(destFile).use { output ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }

            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                sourcePath = sourceUri.toString(),
                destPath = destFile.absolutePath,
                size = destFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import file", e)
            TransferResult(
                success = false,
                sourcePath = sourceUri.toString(),
                destPath = destPath,
                size = 0,
                message = e.message ?: "Unknown error"
            )
        }
    }

    fun exportFile(sourcePath: String, destUri: Uri): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val sourceFile = File(sourcePath)
            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(
                    success = false,
                    sourcePath = sourcePath,
                    destPath = destUri.toString(),
                    size = 0,
                    message = "Source file not found"
                )
            }

            context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(destUri)?.use { output ->
                FileInputStream(sourceFile).use { input ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }

            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                sourcePath = sourcePath,
                destPath = destUri.toString(),
                size = sourceFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to export file", e)
            TransferResult(
                success = false,
                sourcePath = sourcePath,
                destPath = destUri.toString(),
                size = 0,
                message = e.message ?: "Unknown error"
            )
        }
    }

    fun importFileToVm(filePath: String, instanceId: Int, virtualPath: String): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val sourceFile = File(filePath)
            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(
                    success = false,
                    sourcePath = filePath,
                    destPath = virtualPath,
                    size = 0,
                    message = "Source file not found"
                )
            }

            val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
            val destFile = File(vmDir, virtualPath)
            destFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()

            sourceFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)

            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                sourcePath = filePath,
                destPath = destFile.absolutePath,
                size = destFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import file to VM", e)
            TransferResult(
                success = false,
                sourcePath = filePath,
                destPath = virtualPath,
                size = 0,
                message = e.message ?: "Unknown error"
            )
        }
    }

    fun exportFileFromVm(virtualPath: String, destPath: String, instanceId: Int): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
            val sourceFile = File(vmDir, virtualPath)
            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(
                    success = false,
                    sourcePath = virtualPath,
                    destPath = destPath,
                    size = 0,
                    message = "Virtual file not found"
                )
            }

            val destFile = File(destPath)
            destFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()

            sourceFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)

            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                sourcePath = sourceFile.absolutePath,
                destPath = destPath,
                size = destFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to export file from VM", e)
            TransferResult(
                success = false,
                sourcePath = virtualPath,
                destPath = destPath,
                size = 0,
                message = e.message ?: "Unknown error"
            )
        }
    }

    fun listVmFiles(instanceId: Int, subPath: String = ""): List<File> {
        val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
        val targetDir = if (subPath.isEmpty()) vmDir else File(vmDir, subPath)

        return if (targetDir.exists() && targetDir.isDirectory) {
            targetDir.listFiles()?.toList() ?: emptyList()
        } else {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    fun deleteVmFile(instanceId: Int, virtualPath: String): Boolean {
        val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
        val file = File(vmDir, virtualPath)
        return file.delete()
    }

    fun getVmStorageInfo(instanceId: Int): Pair<Long, Long> {
        val vmDir = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$instanceId/data")
        if (!vmDir.exists()) return Pair(0L, 0L)

        var totalSize = 0L
        var fileCount = 0L

        vmDir.walkTopDown().forEach { file ->
            if (file.isFile) {
                totalSize += file.length()
                fileCount++
            }
        }

        return Pair(totalSize, fileCount)
    }
}
```

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.view.Surface

object NativeEngine {

    init {
        System.loadLibrary("vmcore")
    }

    external fun nativeInit(): Int
    external fun nativeDestroy()
    external fun nativeCreateInstance(rootDir: String, dataDir: String): Int
    external fun nativeStartInstance(instanceId: Int): Int
    external fun nativeStopInstance(instanceId: Int): Int
    external fun nativeDestroyInstance(instanceId: Int): Int
    external fun nativeIsInstanceRunning(instanceId: Int): Boolean
    external fun nativeUpdateRootSwitch(instanceId: Int, enable: Boolean): Int
    external fun nativeExtractRom(romPath: String, targetDir: String): Int
    external fun nativeGetEngineVersion(): String
    external fun nativeSetupDisplay(instanceId: Int, surface: Surface?, width: Int, height: Int): Int
    external fun nativeRenderFrame(instanceId: Int): Int
    external fun nativeSendTouchEvent(instanceId: Int, action: Int, x: Int, y: Int, pointerId: Int): Int
    external fun nativeSendKeyEvent(instanceId: Int, keyCode: Int): Int
    external fun nativeSetupVpn(instanceId: Int, fd: Int): Int
    external fun nativeCheckVulkanAvailable(): Int
    external fun nativeSendSensorData(instanceId: Int, sensorType: Int, values: FloatArray): Int
    external fun nativeSendCameraFrame(instanceId: Int, frameData: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): Int

    fun init(): Int = nativeInit()
    fun destroy() = nativeDestroy()
    fun createInstance(rootDir: String, dataDir: String): Int = nativeCreateInstance(rootDir, dataDir)
    fun startInstance(instanceId: Int): Int = nativeStartInstance(instanceId)
    fun stopInstance(instanceId: Int): Int = nativeStopInstance(instanceId)
    fun destroyInstance(instanceId: Int): Int = nativeDestroyInstance(instanceId)
    fun isInstanceRunning(instanceId: Int): Boolean = nativeIsInstanceRunning(instanceId)
    fun updateRootSwitch(instanceId: Int, enable: Boolean): Int = nativeUpdateRootSwitch(instanceId, enable)
    fun extractRom(romPath: String, targetDir: String): Int = nativeExtractRom(romPath, targetDir)
    fun getEngineVersion(): String = nativeGetEngineVersion()
    fun setupDisplay(instanceId: Int, surface: Surface?, width: Int, height: Int): Int = nativeSetupDisplay(instanceId, surface, width, height)
    fun renderFrame(instanceId: Int): Int = nativeRenderFrame(instanceId)
    fun sendTouchEvent(instanceId: Int, action: Int, x: Int, y: Int, pointerId: Int): Int = nativeSendTouchEvent(instanceId, action, x, y, pointerId)
    fun sendKeyEvent(instanceId: Int, keyCode: Int): Int = nativeSendKeyEvent(instanceId, keyCode)
    fun setupVpn(instanceId: Int, fd: Int): Int = nativeSetupVpn(instanceId, fd)
    fun isVulkanAvailable(): Boolean = nativeCheckVulkanAvailable() == 1
    fun sendSensorData(instanceId: Int, sensorType: Int, values: FloatArray): Int = nativeSendSensorData(instanceId, sensorType, values)
    fun sendCameraFrame(instanceId: Int, frameData: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): Int = nativeSendCameraFrame(instanceId, frameData, width, height)
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/SensorManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager as AndroidSensorManager

class GuestSensorManager(context: Context) : SensorEventListener {

    companion object {
        const val SENSOR_TYPE_GYROSCOPE = 4
        const val SENSOR_TYPE_ACCELEROMETER = 1
        const val SENSOR_TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD = 2
        const val SENSOR_TYPE_PROXIMITY = 8
        const val SENSOR_TYPE_LIGHT = 5
    }

    private val sensorManager = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as AndroidSensorManager
    private val sensorData = mutableMapOf<Int, FloatArray>()
    private val listeners = mutableListOf<SensorDataListener>()
    private var isActive = false

    interface SensorDataListener {
        fun onSensorData(sensorType: Int, data: FloatArray, timestamp: Long)
    }

    fun start() {
        if (isActive) return
        isActive = true

        val sensorTypes = intArrayOf(
            SENSOR_TYPE_GYROSCOPE,
            SENSOR_TYPE_ACCELEROMETER,
            SENSOR_TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD,
            SENSOR_TYPE_PROXIMITY,
            SENSOR_TYPE_LIGHT
        )

        sensorTypes.forEach { type ->
            sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(type)?.let { sensor ->
                sensorManager.registerListener(this, sensor, AndroidSensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_GAME)
            }
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        isActive = false
        sensorManager.unregisterListener(this)
    }

    fun registerListener(listener: SensorDataListener) {
        listeners.add(listener)
    }

    fun unregisterListener(listener: SensorDataListener) {
        listeners.remove(listener)
    }

    override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
        val data = event.values.copyOf()
        sensorData[event.sensor.type] = data
        listeners.forEach { it.onSensorData(event.sensor.type, data, event.timestamp) }
    }

    override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor, accuracy: Int) {}

    fun getGyroscopeData(): FloatArray = sensorData[SENSOR_TYPE_GYROSCOPE] ?: FloatArray(3)
    fun getAccelerometerData(): FloatArray = sensorData[SENSOR_TYPE_ACCELEROMETER] ?: FloatArray(3)
    fun getMagneticFieldData(): FloatArray = sensorData[SENSOR_TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD] ?: FloatArray(3)

    fun getSensorData(sensorType: Int): FloatArray = sensorData[sensorType] ?: FloatArray(0)

    fun isSensorAvailable(sensorType: Int): Boolean {
        return sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(sensorType) != null
    }
}
```
