`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/core/VmConfig.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core

import android.content.Context
import java.io.File

data class VmConfig(
    val id: Int = -1,
    var name: String = "VM",
    var androidVersion: Int = 7,
    var screenWidth: Int = 720,
    var screenHeight: Int = 1280,
    var screenDpi: Int = 320,
    var memoryMb: Int = 2048,
    var cpuCount: Int = 2,
    var rootEnabled: Boolean = true,
    var xposedEnabled: Boolean = false,
    var googlePlayEnabled: Boolean = false,
    var createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    var rootDir: String = "",
    var dataDir: String = ""
) {
    fun getRootDir(context: Context): File = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$id/root")
    fun getDataDir(context: Context): File = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$id/data")
    fun getRomDir(context: Context): File = File(context.filesDir, "vm/$id/rom")

    fun toJson(): String {
        return """
            {
                "id": $id,
                "name": "$name",
                "androidVersion": $androidVersion,
                "screenWidth": $screenWidth,
                "screenHeight": $screenHeight,
                "screenDpi": $screenDpi,
                "memoryMb": $memoryMb,
                "cpuCount": $cpuCount,
                "rootEnabled": $rootEnabled,
                "xposedEnabled": $xposedEnabled,
                "googlePlayEnabled": $googlePlayEnabled,
                "createdAt": $createdAt
            }
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    companion object {
        fun fromJson(json: String): VmConfig {
            // Simple JSON parsing
            val map = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
            json.replace("{", "").replace("}", "").split(",").forEach { pair ->
                val kv = pair.split(":").map { it.trim().replace("\"", "") }
                if (kv.size == 2) map[kv[0]] = kv[1]
            }
            return VmConfig(
                id = map["id"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: -1,
                name = map["name"] ?: "VM",
                androidVersion = map["androidVersion"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 7,
                screenWidth = map["screenWidth"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 720,
                screenHeight = map["screenHeight"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 1280,
                screenDpi = map["screenDpi"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 320,
                memoryMb = map["memoryMb"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 2048,
                cpuCount = map["cpuCount"]?.toIntOrNull() ?: 2,
                rootEnabled = map["rootEnabled"]?.toBooleanStrictOrNull() ?: true,
                xposedEnabled = map["xposedEnabled"]?.toBooleanStrictOrNull() ?: false,
                googlePlayEnabled = map["googlePlayEnabled"]?.toBooleanStrictOrNull() ?: false,
                createdAt = map["createdAt"]?.toLongOrNull() ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/google/GoogleServicesInstaller.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.google

import android.app.Dialog
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.drawable.ColorDrawable
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.Window
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.ProgressBar
import android.widget.TextView
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.R

class GoogleServicesInstaller(
    context: Context,
    private val vmRootPath: String,
    private val callback: InstallResultCallback
) : Dialog(context) {

    interface InstallResultCallback {
        fun onInstallComplete(success: Boolean)
    }

    private lateinit var progressBar: ProgressBar
    private lateinit var progressText: TextView
    private lateinit var statusText: TextView
    private lateinit var installButton: Button
    private lateinit var cancelButton: Button
    private val manager = GoogleServicesManager(context)
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE)
        setContentView(R.layout.dialog_google_install)

        window?.apply {
            setBackgroundDrawable(ColorDrawable(android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT))
            setLayout(
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
            setGravity(Gravity.CENTER)
        }

        setCancelable(false)
        initViews()
    }

    private fun initViews() {
        progressBar = findViewById(R.id.progressBar)
        progressText = findViewById(R.id.progressText)
        statusText = findViewById(R.id.statusText)
        installButton = findViewById(R.id.installButton)
        cancelButton = findViewById(R.id.cancelButton)

        installButton.setOnClickListener {
            startInstallation()
        }

        cancelButton.setOnClickListener {
            dismiss()
            callback.onInstallComplete(false)
        }

        updateStatus()
    }

    private fun updateStatus() {
        val statusList = manager.getInstallStatus()
        val installed = statusList.count { it.isInstalled }
        val total = statusList.size

        progressText.text = "$installed / $total"
        statusText.text = statusList.joinToString("\n") { status ->
            val icon = if (status.isInstalled) "✓" else "○"
            "$icon ${status.packageName}"
        }

        installButton.text = if (installed == total) "完成" else "安装"
    }

    private fun startInstallation() {
        installButton.isEnabled = false
        cancelButton.isEnabled = false

        manager.setInstallCallback(object : GoogleServicesManager.InstallCallback {
            override fun onProgress(serviceName: String, progress: Int) {
                handler.post {
                    statusText.text = "正在安装: $serviceName..."
                }
            }

            override fun onComplete(serviceName: String, success: Boolean) {
                handler.post {
                    val icon = if (success) "✓" else "✗"
                    statusText.text = "$icon $serviceName"
                }
            }

            override fun onAllComplete(success: Boolean) {
                handler.post {
                    installButton.isEnabled = true
                    cancelButton.isEnabled = true
                    updateStatus()

                    if (success) {
                        installButton.text = "完成"
                        installButton.setOnClickListener {
                            dismiss()
                            callback.onInstallComplete(true)
                        }
                    } else {
                        statusText.text = "部分安装失败，请重试"
                    }
                }
            }
        })

        Thread {
            manager.installGoogleServices(vmRootPath)
        }.start()
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/google/GoogleServicesManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.google

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

class GoogleServicesManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "GoogleServicesManager"

        private const val GMS_PACKAGE = "com.google.android.gms"
        private const val GSF_PACKAGE = "com.google.android.gsf"
        private const val PLAY_STORE_PACKAGE = "com.android.vending"

        private const val GMS_APK_URL = "https://dl.google.com/dl/android/gms/googleplaygames.apk"
        private const val GSF_APK_URL = "https://dl.google.com/dl/android/gms/googleservicesframework.apk"
        private const val PLAY_STORE_APK_URL = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.android.vending"

        private val REQUIRED_APKS = listOf(
            GoogleServiceInfo(GMS_PACKAGE, "Google Play Services", "gms.apk"),
            GoogleServiceInfo(GSF_PACKAGE, "Google Services Framework", "gsf.apk"),
            GoogleServiceInfo(PLAY_STORE_PACKAGE, "Google Play Store", "playstore.apk")
        )
    }

    data class GoogleServiceInfo(
        val packageName: String,
        val name: String,
        val fileName: String
    )

    data class InstallStatus(
        val packageName: String,
        val isInstalled: Boolean,
        val version: String? = null
    )

    private var installCallback: InstallCallback? = null

    interface InstallCallback {
        fun onProgress(serviceName: String, progress: Int)
        fun onComplete(serviceName: String, success: Boolean)
        fun onAllComplete(success: Boolean)
    }

    fun setInstallCallback(callback: InstallCallback) {
        this.installCallback = callback
    }

    fun isGoogleServicesAvailable(): Boolean {
        return isPackageInstalled(GMS_PACKAGE) &&
               isPackageInstalled(GSF_PACKAGE) &&
               isPackageInstalled(PLAY_STORE_PACKAGE)
    }

    fun getInstallStatus(): List<InstallStatus> {
        return REQUIRED_APKS.map { service ->
            InstallStatus(
                packageName = service.packageName,
                isInstalled = isPackageInstalled(service.packageName),
                version = getPackageVersion(service.packageName)
            )
        }
    }

    fun installGoogleServices(vmRootPath: String): Boolean {
        Log.i(TAG, "Installing Google Services to: $vmRootPath")

        val gmsDir = File(vmRootPath, "system/gms")
        gmsDir.mkdirs()

        var allSuccess = true

        for (service in REQUIRED_APKS) {
            installCallback?.onProgress(service.name, 0)

            val success = installService(service, vmRootPath)
            installCallback?.onComplete(service.name, success)

            if (!success) {
                allSuccess = false
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to install: ${service.name}")
            }
        }

        if (allSuccess) {
            setupGoogleServicesConfig(vmRootPath)
            Log.i(TAG, "Google Services installed successfully")
        }

        installCallback?.onAllComplete(allSuccess)
        return allSuccess
    }

    private fun installService(service: GoogleServiceInfo, vmRootPath: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            val targetDir = File(vmRootPath, "system/app/${service.name}")
            targetDir.mkdirs()

            val apkFile = File(targetDir, service.fileName)

            if (apkFile.exists()) {
                Log.i(TAG, "${service.name} already exists")
                return true
            }

            val assetPath = "google/${service.fileName}"
            try {
                context.assets.open(assetPath).use { input ->
                    FileOutputStream(apkFile).use { output ->
                        input.copyTo(output)
                    }
                }
                Log.i(TAG, "${service.name} installed from assets")
                return true
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Asset not found: $assetPath")
            }

            Log.w(TAG, "Please manually install: ${service.name}")
            false
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to install ${service.name}", e)
            false
        }
    }

    private fun setupGoogleServicesConfig(vmRootPath: String) {
        val configDir = File(vmRootPath, "system/etc")
        configDir.mkdirs()

        val gmsConfig = File(configDir, "gms.conf")
        gmsConfig.writeText("""
# Google Services Configuration
gms.enabled=true
gms.version=1
gms.auto_update=true
gms.push_enabled=true
gms.location_enabled=true
gms.ads_enabled=false
        """.trimIndent())

        val propsFile = File(vmRootPath, "system/build.prop")
        if (!propsFile.exists()) {
            propsFile.writeText("""
ro.build.version.sdk=31
ro.build.version.release=12
ro.product.model=Pixel 6
ro.product.brand=google
ro.product.name=oriole
ro.build.display.id=SP2A.220405.004
ro.com.google.gmsversion=12
            """.trimIndent())
        } else {
            propsFile.appendText("\nro.com.google.gmsversion=12\n")
        }

        Log.i(TAG, "Google Services config setup complete")
    }

    private fun isPackageInstalled(packageName: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0)
            true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun getPackageVersion(packageName: String): String? {
        return try {
            val packageInfo = context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0)
            packageInfo.versionName
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    fun downloadService(service: GoogleServiceInfo, callback: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        Thread {
            try {
                val url = URL(getDownloadUrl(service))
                val connection = url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
                connection.connectTimeout = 30000
                connection.readTimeout = 30000

                if (connection.responseCode == 200) {
                    val cacheDir = File(context.cacheDir, "google_services")
                    cacheDir.mkdirs()
                    val file = File(cacheDir, service.fileName)

                    connection.inputStream.use { input ->
                        FileOutputStream(file).use { output ->
                            input.copyTo(output)
                        }
                    }

                    callback(true)
                } else {
                    callback(false)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Download failed: ${service.name}", e)
                callback(false)
            }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun getDownloadUrl(service: GoogleServiceInfo): String {
        return when (service.packageName) {
            GMS_PACKAGE -> GMS_APK_URL
            GSF_PACKAGE -> GSF_APK_URL
            PLAY_STORE_PACKAGE -> PLAY_STORE_APK_URL
            else -> ""
        }
    }

    fun removeGoogleServices(vmRootPath: String) {
        for (service in REQUIRED_APKS) {
            val targetDir = File(vmRootPath, "system/app/${service.name}")
            if (targetDir.exists()) {
                targetDir.deleteRecursively()
                Log.i(TAG, "Removed: ${service.name}")
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/root/RootManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.root

import android.util.Log
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.NativeEngine
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmInstance
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager

class RootManager(private val vmManager: VmManager) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "RootManager"
    }

    fun enableRoot(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return false
        Log.i(TAG, "Enabling root for instance: $instanceId")

        // Update native root switch
        val result = NativeEngine.updateRootSwitch(instance.nativeId, true)
        if (result != 0) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to update root switch")
            return false
        }

        // Update config
        instance.config.rootEnabled = true

        // TODO: Inject su binary
        // TODO: Install SuperSU
        // TODO: Configure SELinux

        Log.i(TAG, "Root enabled for instance: $instanceId")
        return true
    }

    fun disableRoot(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return false
        Log.i(TAG, "Disabling root for instance: $instanceId")

        val result = NativeEngine.updateRootSwitch(instance.nativeId, false)
        if (result != 0) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to update root switch")
            return false
        }

        instance.config.rootEnabled = false
        Log.i(TAG, "Root disabled for instance: $instanceId")
        return true
    }

    fun isRootEnabled(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        return vmManager.getInstance(instanceId)?.config?.rootEnabled ?: false
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/sensor/SensorManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.sensor

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager as AndroidSensorManager
import android.util.Log

class GuestSensorManager(private val context: Context) : SensorEventListener {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "GuestSensorManager"
    }

    private val sensorManager = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as AndroidSensorManager
    private var listener: SensorDataListener? = null
    private val activeSensors = mutableMapOf<Int, Sensor>()

    interface SensorDataListener {
        fun onSensorData(type: Int, values: FloatArray, timestamp: Long)
    }

    fun setListener(listener: SensorDataListener) {
        this.listener = listener
    }

    fun enableGyroscope(): Boolean {
        return enableSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE)
    }

    fun enableAccelerometer(): Boolean {
        return enableSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER)
    }

    fun enableMagneticField(): Boolean {
        return enableSensor(Sensor.TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD)
    }

    fun enableProximity(): Boolean {
        return enableSensor(Sensor.TYPE_PROXIMITY)
    }

    fun enableLight(): Boolean {
        return enableSensor(Sensor.TYPE_LIGHT)
    }

    fun enableAllSensors() {
        enableGyroscope()
        enableAccelerometer()
        enableMagneticField()
        enableProximity()
        enableLight()
        Log.i(TAG, "All sensors enabled")
    }

    private fun enableSensor(type: Int): Boolean {
        val sensor = sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(type)
        if (sensor != null) {
            sensorManager.registerListener(this, sensor, AndroidSensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_GAME)
            activeSensors[type] = sensor
            Log.i(TAG, "Sensor enabled: ${sensor.name}")
            return true
        }
        Log.w(TAG, "Sensor type $type not available")
        return false
    }

    fun disableSensor(type: Int) {
        activeSensors[type]?.let { sensor ->
            sensorManager.unregisterListener(this, sensor)
            activeSensors.remove(type)
            Log.i(TAG, "Sensor disabled: ${sensor.name}")
        }
    }

    fun disableAllSensors() {
        sensorManager.unregisterListener(this)
        activeSensors.clear()
        Log.i(TAG, "All sensors disabled")
    }

    override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
        listener?.onSensorData(event.sensor.type, event.values.clone(), event.timestamp)
    }

    override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor, accuracy: Int) {
        Log.d(TAG, "Accuracy changed: ${sensor.name} -> $accuracy")
    }

    fun getAvailableSensors(): List<SensorInfo> {
        val sensors = mutableListOf<SensorInfo>()
        val types = listOf(
            Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE,
            Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER,
            Sensor.TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD,
            Sensor.TYPE_PROXIMITY,
            Sensor.TYPE_LIGHT,
            Sensor.TYPE_GRAVITY,
            Sensor.TYPE_LINEAR_ACCELERATION,
            Sensor.TYPE_ROTATION_VECTOR
        )

        for (type in types) {
            sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(type)?.let { sensor ->
                sensors.add(SensorInfo(
                    type = type,
                    name = sensor.name,
                    vendor = sensor.vendor,
                    maxRange = sensor.maximumRange,
                    resolution = sensor.resolution,
                    power = sensor.power
                ))
            }
        }
        return sensors
    }

    fun isSensorAvailable(type: Int): Boolean {
        return sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(type) != null
    }

    data class SensorInfo(
        val type: Int,
        val name: String,
        val vendor: String,
        val maxRange: Float,
        val resolution: Float,
        val power: Float
    )
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/sensor/SensorService.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.sensor

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log

class SensorService(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "SensorService"
    }

    private val nativeSensor = NativeSensor()
    private val guestSensorManager = GuestSensorManager(context)
    private var isRunning = false
    private var sensorCallback: SensorCallback? = null

    interface SensorCallback {
        fun onGyroscopeData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float)
        fun onAccelerometerData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float)
        fun onMagneticFieldData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float)
    }

    fun setCallback(callback: SensorCallback) {
        this.sensorCallback = callback
    }

    fun start(): Boolean {
        if (isRunning) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Sensor service already running")
            return true
        }

        val gyroscopeEnabled = nativeSensor.enableGyroscope()
        val accelerometerEnabled = nativeSensor.enableAccelerometer()
        val magneticFieldEnabled = nativeSensor.enableMagneticField()

        if (!gyroscopeEnabled && !accelerometerEnabled && !magneticFieldEnabled) {
            Log.e(TAG, "No sensors available")
            return false
        }

        isRunning = true
        Log.i(TAG, "Sensor service started (gyro=$gyroscopeEnabled, accel=$accelerometerEnabled, mag=$magneticFieldEnabled)")

        startPollingThread()
        return true
    }

    private fun startPollingThread() {
        Thread {
            val values = FloatArray(3)
            while (isRunning) {
                if (nativeSensor.pollSensorEvent(values)) {
                    val type = nativeSensor.getSensorType()
                    when (type) {
                        4 -> sensorCallback?.onGyroscopeData(values[0], values[1], values[2])
                        1 -> sensorCallback?.onAccelerometerData(values[0], values[1], values[2])
                        2 -> sensorCallback?.onMagneticFieldData(values[0], values[1], values[2])
                    }
                }
                Thread.sleep(16) // ~60fps
            }
        }.start()
    }

    fun stop() {
        isRunning = false
        nativeSensor.disableAllSensors()
        Log.i(TAG, "Sensor service stopped")
    }

    fun isRunning(): Boolean = isRunning

    fun getAvailableSensors(): List<GuestSensorManager.SensorInfo> {
        return guestSensorManager.getAvailableSensors()
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/transfer/FileTransferManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.transfer

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Environment
import android.util.Log
import java.io.*
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

class FileTransferManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "FileTransferManager"
        private const val SHARED_DIR = "VMStudio/Shared"
    }

    data class TransferResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val message: String,
        val filePath: String? = null,
        val fileSize: Long = 0
    )

    fun importFile(uri: Uri): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val inputStream = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)
                ?: return TransferResult(false, "Failed to open input stream")

            val fileName = getFileNameFromUri(uri) ?: "imported_file"
            val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
            val importFile = File(sharedDir, fileName)

            inputStream.use { input ->
                importFile.outputStream().use { output ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }

            Log.i(TAG, "File imported: ${importFile.absolutePath}")
            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                message = "File imported successfully",
                filePath = importFile.absolutePath,
                fileSize = importFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import file", e)
            TransferResult(false, "Import failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    fun importFileFromPath(sourcePath: String): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val sourceFile = File(sourcePath)
            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(false, "Source file not found")
            }

            val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
            val destFile = File(sharedDir, sourceFile.name)

            sourceFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)

            Log.i(TAG, "File imported from path: ${destFile.absolutePath}")
            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                message = "File imported successfully",
                filePath = destFile.absolutePath,
                fileSize = destFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to import file from path", e)
            TransferResult(false, "Import failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    fun exportFile(fileName: String): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
            val sourceFile = File(sharedDir, fileName)

            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(false, "File not found in shared directory")
            }

            val exportDir = getExportDirectory()
            val exportFile = File(exportDir, fileName)

            sourceFile.copyTo(exportFile, overwrite = true)

            Log.i(TAG, "File exported: ${exportFile.absolutePath}")
            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                message = "File exported successfully",
                filePath = exportFile.absolutePath,
                fileSize = exportFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to export file", e)
            TransferResult(false, "Export failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    fun exportFileToUri(fileName: String, destUri: Uri): TransferResult {
        return try {
            val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
            val sourceFile = File(sharedDir, fileName)

            if (!sourceFile.exists()) {
                return TransferResult(false, "File not found in shared directory")
            }

            val outputStream = context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(destUri)
                ?: return TransferResult(false, "Failed to open output stream")

            sourceFile.inputStream().use { input ->
                outputStream.use { output ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }

            Log.i(TAG, "File exported to URI")
            TransferResult(
                success = true,
                message = "File exported successfully",
                fileSize = sourceFile.length()
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to export file to URI", e)
            TransferResult(false, "Export failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    fun listSharedFiles(): List<FileInfo> {
        val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
        if (!sharedDir.exists()) {
            return emptyList()
        }

        return sharedDir.listFiles()?.map { file ->
            FileInfo(
                name = file.name,
                path = file.absolutePath,
                size = file.length(),
                isDirectory = file.isDirectory,
                lastModified = file.lastModified()
            )
        }?.sortedBy { it.name } ?: emptyList()
    }

    fun deleteSharedFile(fileName: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
            val file = File(sharedDir, fileName)
            if (file.exists()) {
                file.delete()
                Log.i(TAG, "Deleted shared file: $fileName")
                true
            } else {
                false
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to delete shared file", e)
            false
        }
    }

    fun getSharedDirectory(): File {
        val dir = File(
            Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS),
            SHARED_DIR
        )
        dir.mkdirs()
        return dir
    }

    fun getExportDirectory(): File {
        val dir = File(
            Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS),
            "VMStudio/Export"
        )
        dir.mkdirs()
        return dir
    }

    fun getStorageInfo(): StorageInfo {
        val sharedDir = getSharedDirectory()
        val totalSize = sharedDir.totalSpace
        val usableSize = sharedDir.usableSpace
        val usedSize = totalSize - usableSize

        return StorageInfo(
            totalSpace = totalSize,
            usableSpace = usableSize,
            usedSpace = usedSize,
            sharedFilesCount = listSharedFiles().size
        )
    }

    private fun getFileNameFromUri(uri: Uri): String? {
        var fileName: String? = null
        if (uri.scheme == "content") {
            val cursor = context.contentResolver.query(uri, null, null, null, null)
            cursor?.use {
                if (it.moveToFirst()) {
                    val nameIndex = it.getColumnIndex(android.provider.OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                    if (nameIndex >= 0) {
                        fileName = it.getString(nameIndex)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (fileName == null) {
            fileName = uri.path?.substringAfterLast('/')
        }
        return fileName
    }

    data class FileInfo(
        val name: String,
        val path: String,
        val size: Long,
        val isDirectory: Boolean,
        val lastModified: Long
    )

    data class StorageInfo(
        val totalSpace: Long,
        val usableSpace: Long,
        val usedSpace: Long,
        val sharedFilesCount: Int
    )
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/ui/MainActivity.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui

import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.View
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import androidx.appcompat.widget.Toolbar
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.google.android.material.floatingactionbutton.ExtendedFloatingActionButton
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.R
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager

class MainActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var vmManager: VmManager
    private lateinit var adapter: VmListAdapter
    private lateinit var toolbar: Toolbar
    private lateinit var rvVmList: RecyclerView
    private lateinit var tvEmpty: TextView
    private lateinit var fabCreate: ExtendedFloatingActionButton

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main)

        toolbar = findViewById(R.id.toolbar)
        rvVmList = findViewById(R.id.rvVmList)
        tvEmpty = findViewById(R.id.tvEmpty)
        fabCreate = findViewById(R.id.fabCreate)

        vmManager = VmManager(this)
        setupToolbar()
        setupRecyclerView()
        setupFab()
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        refreshList()
    }

    private fun setupToolbar() {
        setSupportActionBar(toolbar)
    }

    private fun setupRecyclerView() {
        adapter = VmListAdapter(
            instances = vmManager.getAllInstances(),
            onStartClick = { instance ->
                if (instance.isRunning) {
                    vmManager.stopInstance(instance.config.id)
                } else {
                    val intent = Intent(this, VmDisplayActivity::class.java)
                    intent.putExtra("instance_id", instance.config.id)
                    startActivity(intent)
                }
                refreshList()
            },
            onSettingsClick = { instance ->
                val intent = Intent(this, VmSettingsActivity::class.java)
                intent.putExtra("instance_id", instance.config.id)
                startActivity(intent)
            },
            onDeleteClick = { instance ->
                AlertDialog.Builder(this)
                    .setTitle("删除虚拟机")
                    .setMessage("确认删除 ${instance.config.name}？")
                    .setPositiveButton("删除") { _, _ ->
                        vmManager.destroyInstance(instance.config.id)
                        refreshList()
                    }
                    .setNegativeButton("取消", null)
                    .show()
            }
        )
        rvVmList.layoutManager = LinearLayoutManager(this)
        rvVmList.adapter = adapter
    }

    private fun setupFab() {
        fabCreate.setOnClickListener {
            startActivity(Intent(this, VmCreateActivity::class.java))
        }
    }

    private fun refreshList() {
        val instances = vmManager.getAllInstances()
        adapter.updateData(instances)

        if (instances.isEmpty()) {
            tvEmpty.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            rvVmList.visibility = View.GONE
        } else {
            tvEmpty.visibility = View.GONE
            rvVmList.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/ui/VmCreateActivity.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.R
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.VmConfig
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.databinding.ActivityVmCreateBinding
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager

class VmCreateActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var binding: ActivityVmCreateBinding
    private lateinit var vmManager: VmManager

    private val androidVersions = arrayOf("Android 5.1", "Android 7.1.2", "Android 9")
    private val resolutions = arrayOf("720x1280", "1080x1920", "1440x2560")
    private val memories = arrayOf("1024 MB", "2048 MB", "4096 MB")

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        binding = ActivityVmCreateBinding.inflate(layoutInflater)
        setContentView(binding.root)

        vmManager = VmManager(this)
        setupToolbar()
        setupSpinners()
        setupCreateButton()
    }

    private fun setupToolbar() {
        setSupportActionBar(binding.toolbar)
        binding.toolbar.setNavigationOnClickListener { finish() }
    }

    private fun setupSpinners() {
        binding.spinnerAndroidVersion.adapter = ArrayAdapter(
            this, android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, androidVersions
        )
        binding.spinnerAndroidVersion.setSelection(1)

        binding.spinnerResolution.adapter = ArrayAdapter(
            this, android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, resolutions
        )

        binding.spinnerMemory.adapter = ArrayAdapter(
            this, android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, memories
        )
        binding.spinnerMemory.setSelection(1)
    }

    private fun setupCreateButton() {
        binding.btnCreate.setOnClickListener {
            val name = binding.etVmName.text.toString().ifEmpty { "VM" }
            val versionIdx = binding.spinnerAndroidVersion.selectedItemPosition
            val resIdx = binding.spinnerResolution.selectedItemPosition
            val memIdx = binding.spinnerMemory.selectedItemPosition

            val androidVersion = when (versionIdx) {
                0 -> 5
                1 -> 7
                2 -> 9
                else -> 7
            }

            val resolution = resolutions[resIdx].split("x")
            val width = resolution[0].toInt()
            val height = resolution[1].toInt()

            val memory = when (memIdx) {
                0 -> 1024
                1 -> 2048
                2 -> 4096
                else -> 2048
            }

            val config = VmConfig(
                name = name,
                androidVersion = androidVersion,
                screenWidth = width,
                screenHeight = height,
                memoryMb = memory,
                rootEnabled = binding.switchRoot.isChecked,
                xposedEnabled = binding.switchXposed.isChecked
            )

            val result = vmManager.createInstance(config)
            if (result.id > 0) {
                Toast.makeText(this, "虚拟机创建成功", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                finish()
            } else {
                Toast.makeText(this, "创建失败", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/ui/VmDisplayActivity.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.SurfaceHolder
import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.NativeEngine
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.databinding.ActivityVmDisplayBinding
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.sensor.SensorService
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.camera.CameraService
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.background.BackgroundManager

class VmDisplayActivity : AppCompatActivity(), SurfaceHolder.Callback {

    private lateinit var binding: ActivityVmDisplayBinding
    private lateinit var vmManager: VmManager
    private lateinit var sensorService: SensorService
    private lateinit var cameraService: CameraService
    private lateinit var backgroundManager: BackgroundManager
    private var instanceId = -1
    private var isRunning = false
    private var useVulkan = false

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        binding = ActivityVmDisplayBinding.inflate(layoutInflater)
        setContentView(binding.root)

        instanceId = intent.getIntExtra("instance_id", -1)
        if (instanceId < 0) {
            finish()
            return
        }

        vmManager = VmManager(this)
        sensorService = SensorService(this)
        cameraService = CameraService(this)
        backgroundManager = BackgroundManager(this)
        
        // Check Vulkan availability
        useVulkan = NativeEngine.isVulkanAvailable()
        if (useVulkan) {
            android.util.Log.i("VmDisplay", "Using Vulkan renderer")
        } else {
            android.util.Log.i("VmDisplay", "Using OpenGL ES renderer")
        }
        
        setupSurface()
        setupButtons()
        setupServices()
    }

    private fun setupServices() {
        // Setup sensor callback
        sensorService.setCallback(object : SensorService.SensorCallback {
            override fun onGyroscopeData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float) {
                NativeEngine.sendSensorData(instanceId, 4, floatArrayOf(x, y, z))
            }

            override fun onAccelerometerData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float) {
                NativeEngine.sendSensorData(instanceId, 1, floatArrayOf(x, y, z))
            }

            override fun onMagneticFieldData(x: Float, y: Float, z: Float) {
                NativeEngine.sendSensorData(instanceId, 2, floatArrayOf(x, y, z))
            }
        })

        // Setup camera callback
        cameraService.setCallback(object : CameraService.CameraCallback {
            override fun onFrameCaptured(data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int) {
                NativeEngine.sendCameraFrame(instanceId, data, width, height)
            }
        })

        // Keep alive in background
        backgroundManager.keepAlive()
    }

    private fun setupSurface() {
        binding.surfaceView.holder.addCallback(this)
    }

    private fun setupButtons() {
        binding.btnBack.setOnClickListener {
            if (isRunning) {
                AlertDialog.Builder(this)
                    .setTitle("返回")
                    .setMessage("虚拟机仍在运行，返回后将在后台运行")
                    .setPositiveButton("确定") { _, _ -> finish() }
                    .setNegativeButton("取消", null)
                    .show()
            } else {
                finish()
            }
        }

        binding.btnHome.setOnClickListener {
            NativeEngine.sendKeyEvent(instanceId, 3)
        }

        binding.btnRecent.setOnClickListener {
            NativeEngine.sendKeyEvent(instanceId, 187)
        }
        
        // Show renderer info
        val rendererInfo = if (useVulkan) "Vulkan" else "OpenGL ES"
        binding.tvRendererInfo?.text = rendererInfo
    }

    override fun surfaceCreated(holder: SurfaceHolder) {
        NativeEngine.setupDisplay(instanceId, holder.surface, binding.surfaceView.width, binding.surfaceView.height)
        startInstance()
    }

    override fun surfaceChanged(holder: SurfaceHolder, format: Int, width: Int, height: Int) {
        NativeEngine.setupDisplay(instanceId, holder.surface, width, height)
    }

    override fun surfaceDestroyed(holder: SurfaceHolder) {
    }

    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        val x = event.x.toInt()
        val y = event.y.toInt()
        val pointerId = event.getPointerId(event.actionIndex)

        when (event.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN, MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_DOWN -> {
                NativeEngine.sendTouchEvent(instanceId, 0, x, y, pointerId)
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                for (i in 0 until event.pointerCount) {
                    val px = event.getX(i).toInt()
                    val py = event.getY(i).toInt()
                    val pid = event.getPointerId(i)
                    NativeEngine.sendTouchEvent(instanceId, 2, px, py, pid)
                }
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_UP -> {
                NativeEngine.sendTouchEvent(instanceId, 1, x, y, pointerId)
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    private fun startInstance() {
        vmManager.startInstance(instanceId)
        isRunning = true

        // Start sensor service
        sensorService.start()

        // Start camera service
        cameraService.start()

        // Start foreground service
        backgroundManager.startForegroundService(
            com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.background.ForegroundService::class.java
        )
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        super.onPause()
        if (isRunning) {
            sensorService.stop()
            cameraService.stop()
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        if (isRunning) {
            sensorService.start()
            cameraService.start()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        sensorService.stop()
        cameraService.stop()
        if (isRunning) {
            vmManager.stopInstance(instanceId)
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/ui/VmListAdapter.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui

import android.graphics.Color
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageButton
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.R
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmInstance

class VmListAdapter(
    private var instances: List<VmInstance>,
    private val onStartClick: (VmInstance) -> Unit,
    private val onSettingsClick: (VmInstance) -> Unit,
    private val onDeleteClick: (VmInstance) -> Unit
) : RecyclerView.Adapter<VmListAdapter.VmViewHolder>() {

    class VmViewHolder(view: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(view) {
        val tvName: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvVmName)
        val tvInfo: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.tvVmInfo)
        val tvStatus: TextView = view.findViewById(R.id.chipStatus)
        val btnStart: ImageButton = view.findViewById(R.id.btnStart)
        val btnSettings: ImageButton = view.findViewById(R.id.btnSettings)
        val btnDelete: ImageButton = view.findViewById(R.id.btnDelete)
    }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): VmViewHolder {
        val view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
            .inflate(R.layout.item_vm, parent, false)
        return VmViewHolder(view)
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: VmViewHolder, position: Int) {
        val instance = instances[position]
        holder.tvName.text = instance.displayName
        holder.tvInfo.text = instance.info

        when (instance.status) {
            VmInstance.Status.RUNNING -> {
                holder.tvStatus.text = "运行中"
                holder.tvStatus.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#4CAF50"))
                holder.btnStart.setImageResource(android.R.drawable.ic_media_pause)
            }
            VmInstance.Status.READY -> {
                holder.tvStatus.text = "就绪"
                holder.tvStatus.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#FFC107"))
                holder.btnStart.setImageResource(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            }
            else -> {
                holder.tvStatus.text = "已停止"
                holder.tvStatus.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#F44336"))
                holder.btnStart.setImageResource(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            }
        }

        holder.btnStart.setOnClickListener { onStartClick(instance) }
        holder.btnSettings.setOnClickListener { onSettingsClick(instance) }
        holder.btnDelete.setOnClickListener { onDeleteClick(instance) }
    }

    override fun getItemCount() = instances.size

    fun updateData(newInstances: List<VmInstance>) {
        instances = newInstances
        notifyDataSetChanged()
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/ui/VmSettingsActivity.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui

import android.os.Bundle
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.databinding.ActivityVmSettingsBinding
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.root.RootManager
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.xposed.XposedManager
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.google.GoogleServicesManager
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.google.GoogleServicesInstaller

class VmSettingsActivity : AppCompatActivity() {

    private lateinit var binding: ActivityVmSettingsBinding
    private lateinit var vmManager: VmManager
    private lateinit var rootManager: RootManager
    private lateinit var xposedManager: XposedManager
    private lateinit var googleManager: GoogleServicesManager
    private var instanceId = -1

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        binding = ActivityVmSettingsBinding.inflate(layoutInflater)
        setContentView(binding.root)

        instanceId = intent.getIntExtra("instance_id", -1)
        if (instanceId < 0) {
            finish()
            return
        }

        vmManager = VmManager(this)
        rootManager = RootManager(vmManager)
        xposedManager = XposedManager(vmManager)
        googleManager = GoogleServicesManager(this)

        setupToolbar()
        setupSettings()
    }

    private fun setupToolbar() {
        setSupportActionBar(binding.toolbar)
        supportActionBar?.title = "设置"
        binding.toolbar.setNavigationOnClickListener { finish() }
    }

    private fun setupSettings() {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return

        binding.switchRoot.isChecked = instance.config.rootEnabled
        binding.switchXposed.isChecked = instance.config.xposedEnabled

        updateGoogleStatus()

        binding.switchRoot.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, isChecked ->
            if (isChecked) {
                rootManager.enableRoot(instanceId)
            } else {
                rootManager.disableRoot(instanceId)
            }
            Toast.makeText(this, if (isChecked) "Root已启用" else "Root已禁用", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        binding.switchXposed.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, isChecked ->
            if (isChecked) {
                xposedManager.enableXposed(instanceId)
            } else {
                xposedManager.disableXposed(instanceId)
            }
            Toast.makeText(this, if (isChecked) "Xposed已启用" else "Xposed已禁用", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        binding.btnInstallGoogle?.setOnClickListener {
            showGoogleInstaller()
        }

        binding.switchGoogle?.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, isChecked ->
            if (isChecked) {
                showGoogleInstaller()
            } else {
                removeGoogleServices()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateGoogleStatus() {
        val statusList = googleManager.getInstallStatus()
        val installed = statusList.count { it.isInstalled }

        binding.tvGoogleStatus?.text = if (installed == 3) {
            "已安装"
        } else {
            "未安装 ($installed/3)"
        }

        binding.switchGoogle?.isChecked = installed == 3
    }

    private fun showGoogleInstaller() {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return
        val vmRootPath = instance.config.rootDir

        val installer = GoogleServicesInstaller(this, vmRootPath, object : GoogleServicesInstaller.InstallResultCallback {
            override fun onInstallComplete(success: Boolean) {
                runOnUiThread {
                    updateGoogleStatus()
                    if (success) {
                        Toast.makeText(this@VmSettingsActivity, "Google服务安装完成", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                }
            }
        })

        installer.show()
    }

    private fun removeGoogleServices() {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return
        googleManager.removeGoogleServices(instance.config.rootDir)
        updateGoogleStatus()
        Toast.makeText(this, "Google服务已移除", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/vm/VmInstance.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm

import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.VmConfig

data class VmInstance(
    val config: VmConfig,
    val nativeId: Int,
    var status: Status = Status.READY
) {
    enum class Status {
        READY,
        RUNNING,
        STOPPED,
        ERROR
    }

    val isRunning: Boolean get() = status == Status.RUNNING
    val displayName: String get() = config.name
    val info: String get() = "Android ${config.androidVersion} | ${config.screenWidth}x${config.screenHeight} | ${config.memoryMb}MB"
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/vm/VmManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.NativeEngine
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.core.VmConfig
import java.io.File

class VmManager(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "VmManager"
        private const val PREFS_NAME = "vm_prefs"
        private const val KEY_INSTANCES = "instances"
    }

    private val gson = Gson()
    private val instances = mutableMapOf<Int, VmInstance>()
    private var nextId = 1

    init {
        loadInstances()
    }

    fun createInstance(config: VmConfig): VmConfig {
        val id = nextId++
        val newConfig = config.copy(id = id)

        // Create directories
        val rootDir = newConfig.getRootDir(context)
        val dataDir = newConfig.getDataDir(context)
        rootDir.mkdirs()
        dataDir.mkdirs()

        // Create native instance
        val nativeId = NativeEngine.createInstance(rootDir.absolutePath, dataDir.absolutePath)
        if (nativeId < 0) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to create native instance")
            return newConfig.copy(id = -1)
        }

        val instance = VmInstance(
            config = newConfig,
            nativeId = nativeId,
            status = VmInstance.Status.READY
        )
        instances[id] = instance
        saveInstances()

        Log.i(TAG, "Instance created: id=$id name=${newConfig.name}")
        return newConfig
    }

    fun startInstance(id: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = instances[id] ?: return false
        if (instance.status == VmInstance.Status.RUNNING) return true

        val result = NativeEngine.startInstance(instance.nativeId)
        if (result == 0) {
            instance.status = VmInstance.Status.RUNNING
            saveInstances()
            Log.i(TAG, "Instance started: id=$id")
            return true
        }
        Log.e(TAG, "Failed to start instance: id=$id result=$result")
        return false
    }

    fun stopInstance(id: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = instances[id] ?: return false
        if (instance.status != VmInstance.Status.RUNNING) return true

        val result = NativeEngine.stopInstance(instance.nativeId)
        if (result == 0) {
            instance.status = VmInstance.Status.READY
            saveInstances()
            Log.i(TAG, "Instance stopped: id=$id")
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    fun destroyInstance(id: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = instances[id] ?: return false
        stopInstance(id)
        NativeEngine.destroyInstance(instance.nativeId)
        instances.remove(id)

        // Clean up directories
        instance.config.getRootDir(context).deleteRecursively()
        instance.config.getDataDir(context).deleteRecursively()
        instance.config.getRomDir(context).deleteRecursively()

        saveInstances()
        Log.i(TAG, "Instance destroyed: id=$id")
        return true
    }

    fun getInstance(id: Int): VmInstance? = instances[id]

    fun getAllInstances(): List<VmInstance> = instances.values.toList()

    fun updateRootSwitch(id: Int, enable: Boolean): Boolean {
        val instance = instances[id] ?: return false
        val result = NativeEngine.updateRootSwitch(instance.nativeId, enable)
        if (result == 0) {
            instance.config.rootEnabled = enable
            saveInstances()
            Log.i(TAG, "Root switch: id=$id enable=$enable")
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun loadInstances() {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val json = prefs.getString(KEY_INSTANCES, null)
        if (json != null) {
            val type = object : TypeToken<List<VmConfig>>() {}.type
            val configs: List<VmConfig> = try {
                gson.fromJson(json, type)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                emptyList()
            }
            configs.forEach { config ->
                val rootDir = config.getRootDir(context)
                val dataDir = config.getDataDir(context)
                val nativeId = NativeEngine.createInstance(rootDir.absolutePath, dataDir.absolutePath)
                if (nativeId >= 0) {
                    instances[config.id] = VmInstance(
                        config = config,
                        nativeId = nativeId,
                        status = VmInstance.Status.READY
                    )
                    if (config.id >= nextId) nextId = config.id + 1
                }
            }
            Log.i(TAG, "Loaded ${instances.size} instances")
        }
    }

    private fun saveInstances() {
        val configs = instances.values.map { it.config }
        val json = gson.toJson(configs)
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit().putString(KEY_INSTANCES, json).apply()
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/vm/VmService.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Binder
import android.os.IBinder
import android.util.Log
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.R
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.VMApp
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.ui.MainActivity

class VmService : Service() {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "VmService"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1
    }

    private val binder = LocalBinder()
    private var vmManager: VmManager? = null

    inner class LocalBinder : Binder() {
        fun getService(): VmService = this@VmService
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder = binder

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        vmManager = VmManager(this)
        Log.i(TAG, "VmService created")
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            "ACTION_START_VM" -> {
                val instanceId = intent.getIntExtra("instance_id", -1)
                if (instanceId >= 0) {
                    startForeground(instanceId, createNotification(instanceId))
                    vmManager?.startInstance(instanceId)
                }
            }
            "ACTION_STOP_VM" -> {
                val instanceId = intent.getIntExtra("instance_id", -1)
                if (instanceId >= 0) {
                    vmManager?.stopInstance(instanceId)
                    stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
                    stopSelf()
                }
            }
        }
        return START_NOT_STICKY
    }

    private fun createNotification(instanceId: Int): Notification {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java)
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this, 0, intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return Notification.Builder(this, VMApp.CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("VM Studio")
            .setContentText("虚拟机 #$instanceId 运行中")
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_compass)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .build()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        Log.i(TAG, "VmService destroyed")
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/vmstudio/virtualmachine/xposed/XposedManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.xposed

import android.util.Log
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmInstance
import com.vmstudio.virtualmachine.vm.VmManager

class XposedManager(private val vmManager: VmManager) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "XposedManager"
    }

    fun enableXposed(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return false
        Log.i(TAG, "Enabling Xposed for instance: $instanceId")

        instance.config.xposedEnabled = true

        // TODO: Copy XposedBridge.jar
        // TODO: Modify app_process
        // TODO: Configure init.rc

        Log.i(TAG, "Xposed enabled for instance: $instanceId")
        return true
    }

    fun disableXposed(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        val instance = vmManager.getInstance(instanceId) ?: return false
        Log.i(TAG, "Disabling Xposed for instance: $instanceId")

        instance.config.xposedEnabled = false

        // TODO: Remove Xposed files
        // TODO: Restore app_process

        Log.i(TAG, "Xposed disabled for instance: $instanceId")
        return true
    }

    fun isXposedEnabled(instanceId: Int): Boolean {
        return vmManager.getInstance(instanceId)?.config?.xposedEnabled ?: false
    }

    fun getInstalledModules(instanceId: Int): List<XposedModule> {
        // TODO: Scan /data/data/ for Xposed modules
        return emptyList()
    }
}
```

```properties
distributionBase=GRADLE_USER_HOME

distributionUrl=https\://services.gradle.org/distributions/gradle-8.0-bin.zip
```

`gradlew`:

```
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#

##############################################################################
##
##  Gradle start up script for UN*X
##
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME
# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
PRG="$0"
# Need this for relative symlinks.
while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
    ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
    link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
    if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
        PRG="$link"
    else
        PRG=`dirname "$PRG"`"/$link"
    fi
done
SAVED="`pwd`"
cd "`dirname \"$PRG\"`/" >/dev/null
APP_HOME="`pwd -P`"
cd "$SAVED" >/dev/null

APP_NAME="Gradle"
APP_BASE_NAME=`basename "$0"`

# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD="maximum"

warn () {
    echo "$*"
}

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
}

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "`uname`" in
  CYGWIN* )
    cygwin=true
    ;;
  Darwin* )
    darwin=true
    ;;
  MSYS* | MINGW* )
    msys=true
    ;;
  NONSTOP* )
    nonstop=true
    ;;
esac

CLASSPATH=$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar


# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
    else
        JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD="java"
    which java >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if [ "$cygwin" = "false" -a "$darwin" = "false" -a "$nonstop" = "false" ] ; then
    MAX_FD_LIMIT=`ulimit -H -n`
    if [ $? -eq 0 ] ; then
        if [ "$MAX_FD" = "maximum" -o "$MAX_FD" = "max" ] ; then
            MAX_FD="$MAX_FD_LIMIT"
        fi
        ulimit -n $MAX_FD
        if [ $? -ne 0 ] ; then
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit: $MAX_FD"
        fi
    else
        warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit: $MAX_FD_LIMIT"
    fi
fi

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if [ "$cygwin" = "true" -o "$msys" = "true" ] ; then
    APP_HOME=`cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME"`
    CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --mixed "$CLASSPATH"`
    JAVACMD=`cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD"`

    # We build the pattern for arguments to be converted via cygpath
    ROOTDIRSRAW=`find -L / -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d 2>/dev/null`
    SEP=""
    for dir in $ROOTDIRSRAW ; do
        ROOTDIRS="$ROOTDIRS$SEP$dir"
        SEP="|"
    done
    OURCYGPATTERN="(^($ROOTDIRS))"
    # Add a user-defined pattern to the cygpath arguments
    if [ "$GRADLE_CYGPATTERN" != "" ] ; then
        OURCYGPATTERN="$OURCYGPATTERN|($GRADLE_CYGPATTERN)"
    fi
    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    i=0
    for arg in "$@" ; do
        CHECK=`echo "$arg"|egrep -c "$OURCYGPATTERN" -`
        CHECK2=`echo "$arg"|egrep -c "^-"`                                 ### Annoying, but necessary
        if [ $CHECK -ne 0 ] && [ $CHECK2 -eq 0 ] ; then
            eval `echo args$i`=`cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg"`
        else
            eval `echo args$i`="\"$arg\""
        fi
        i=`expr $i + 1`
    done
    case $i in
        0) set -- ;;
        1) set -- "$args0" ;;
        2) set -- "$args0" "$args1" ;;
        3) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" ;;
        4) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" ;;
        5) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" "$args4" ;;
        6) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" "$args4" "$args5" ;;
        7) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" "$args4" "$args5" "$args6" ;;
        8) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" "$args4" "$args5" "$args6" "$args7" ;;
        9) set -- "$args0" "$args1" "$args2" "$args3" "$args4" "$args5" "$args6" "$args7" "$args8" ;;
    esac
fi

# Escape application args
save () {
    for i do printf %s\\n "$i" | sed "s/'/eli__NL__'/g" ; done
    unset i
}
APP_ARGS=`save "$@"`

# Collect all arguments for the java command, following the shell quoting and substitution rules
eval set -- $DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS "\"-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME\"" -classpath "\"$CLASSPATH\"" org.gradle.wrapper.GradleWrapperMain "$APP_ARGS"

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

`settings.gradle.kts`:

```kts
pluginManagement {
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
        gradlePluginPortal()
    }
}
dependencyResolutionManagement {
    repositoriesMode.set(RepositoriesMode.FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS)
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
    }
}

rootProject.name = "VirtualMachine"
include(":app")
```
